Taken together, these results show that a single end-to-end policy can deliver multi-modal, contact aware humanoid recovery that generalizes across perturbation magnitude, contact geometry, and dynamics shift.

</details>


### [218] [Collaborative Trajectory Prediction via Late Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22973)
*Nadya Abdel Madjid,Murad Mebrahtu,Zakhar Yagudin,Bilal Hassan,Naoufel Werghi,Jorge Dias,Dzmitry Tsetserukou,Majid Khonji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向V2V协同轨迹预测的晚期融合框架，将协作从感知阶段转移到预测模块，通过共享预测结果而非高维特征图来降低通信开销，并在多个数据集上验证了该方法相比独立预测的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同轨迹预测方法在感知阶段融合特征图构建全局视角，但存在通信开销大、依赖理想化带宽和同步假设的问题，限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出晚期融合框架，将协作从感知层转移到预测模块，各车辆独立进行预测后共享预测结果进行融合，框架模型无关且支持异步协作。

Result: 在OPV2V、V2V4Real、DeepAccident数据集上，晚期融合相比独立预测持续降低漏检率并提升轨迹成功率；在V2V4Real上，协同预测使两辆智能车的成功率分别提升1.69%和1.22%。

Conclusion: 将协作从感知阶段转移到预测模块的晚期融合策略，能在降低通信开销的同时有效提升轨迹预测性能，更具实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Predicting future trajectories of surrounding traffic agents is critical for safe autonomous navigation and collision avoidance. Despite all advances in the trajectory forecasting realm, the prediction models remains vulnerable to uncertainty caused by occlusions, limited sensing range, and perception errors. Collaborative vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) approaches help reduce this uncertainty by sharing complementary information. Existing collaborative trajectory prediction methods typically fuse feature maps at the perception stage to construct a holistic scene view. Further this holistic representation is decoded into the future trajectories. Such design incurs substantial communication overhead due to the exchange of high-dimensional feature representations and often assumes idealized bandwidth and synchronization, limiting practical deployment. We address these limitations by shifting collaboration from perception to the prediction module and introducing a late-fusion framework for shared forecasts. The framework is model-agnostic and treats collaborating vehicles as independent asynchronous agents. We evaluate the approach on the OPV2V, V2V4Real, and DeepAccident datasets, comparing individual and collaborative forecasting. Across all datasets, late fusion consistently reduces miss rate and improves trajectory success rate ($\mathrm{TSR}_{0.5}$), defined as the fraction of ground-truth agents with final displacement error below 0.5 m. On the real-world V2V4Real dataset, collaborative prediction improves the success rate by $1.69\%$ and $1.22\%$ for both intelligent vehicles, respectively, compared with individual forecasting.

</details>


### [219] [Learning from the Best: Smoothness-Driven Metrics for Data Quality in Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23000)
*Soham Kulkarni,Raayan Dhar,Yuchen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RINSE框架，利用轨迹平滑度（SAL和TED指标）对演示数据进行质量评分，无需训练策略即可筛选高质量数据，在多个基准和真实场景中提升行为克隆性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准行为克隆平等对待所有演示数据，但真实数据质量参差不齐，现有筛选方法需要策略训练或人工标注，成本高昂难以扩展。

Method: 基于运动控制理论中平滑度表征熟练运动的原理，提出两种互补指标：SAL（频谱弧长，频域规则性）和TED（轨迹-包络距离，接触感知几何偏差）。通过平滑度过滤降低保留数据的条件动作方差，支持过滤、检索、重加权三种应用模式。

Result: RoboMimic上SAL过滤用1/6数据提升16%成功率；真实操控中TED过滤用一半数据提升20%；LIBERO-10上检索重排序提升5.6%；Re-Mix域重加权中与学习分配的Spearman ρ≥0.89。

Conclusion: 轨迹平滑度是实用的数据质量信号，在过滤、检索、重加权场景中均有效，尤其适用于噪声大或异构的数据场景。

Abstract: In behavioral cloning (BC), policy performance is fundamentally limited by demonstration data quality. Real-world datasets contain trajectories of varying quality due to operator skill differences, teleoperation artifacts, and procedural inconsistencies, yet standard BC treats all demonstrations equally. Existing curation methods require costly policy training in the loop or manual annotation, limiting scalability. We propose RINSE (Ranking and INdexing Smooth Examples), a lightweight framework for scoring demonstrations based on trajectory smoothness that is policy-architecture-agnostic and operates on trajectory data alone, with TED additionally using a phase-boundary/contact signal. Grounded in motor control theory, which establishes smoothness as a hallmark of skilled movement, RINSE uses two complementary metrics: Spectral Arc Length (SAL), a spectral measure of frequency-domain regularity, and Trajectory-Envelope Distance (TED), a spatial measure of contact-aware geometric deviation. We show that smoothness filtering can reduce the conditional action variance of the retained data distribution, with downstream effects that can be amplified by action chunking and compounding error. On RoboMimic benchmarks, SAL filtering achieves 16% higher success using one-sixth of the data. On real-world manipulation, TED filtering achieves 20% improvement with half the data. As a retrieval-stage filter within STRAP on LIBERO-10, RINSE re-ranking improves mean success by 5.6%. As soft weights in Re-Mix domain reweighting, RINSE scores produce domain allocations highly correlated with the learned Re-Mix allocations (Spearman $ρ\geq 0.89$). These results support smoothness as a useful quality signal across filtering, retrieval, and reweighting settings, especially in noisy or heterogeneous data regimes.

</details>


### [220] [Vision-Language-Action in Robotics: A Survey of Datasets, Benchmarks, and Data Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23001)
*Ziyao Wang,Bingying Wang,Hanrong Zhang,Tingting Du,Tianyang Chen,Guoheng Sun,Yexiao He,Zheyu Shen,Wanghao Ye,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型进行数据导向的系统性综述，围绕数据集、基准测试和数据引擎三大支柱展开分析，指出VLA未来进展的关键在于高质量数据基础设施与结构化评估协议的协同设计，而非模型架构本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽取得显著进展，但底层的**数据基础设施**被严重忽视。作者认为未来VLA的瓶颈将不再是模型架构，而是**高保真数据引擎**与**结构化评估协议**的协同设计问题。

Method: 采用**数据导向**的系统综述方法，从三个维度展开：(1) 数据集：对真实世界与合成语料库按具身多样性、模态组成和动作空间制定进行分类；(2) 基准测试：联合分析任务复杂度与环境结构，揭示组合泛化与长程推理评估的结构性缺口；(3) 数据引擎：审视仿真驱动、视频重建和自动化任务生成范式，识别其物理基真性与仿真到现实迁移的共同局限。

Result: 识别出四大开放挑战：(1) 表示对齐（representation alignment）；(2) 多模态监督（multimodal supervision）；(3) 推理评估（reasoning assessment）；(4) 可扩展数据生成（scalable data generation）。同时揭示了一个关键权衡：**保真度-成本权衡**（fidelity-cost trade-off）从根本上限制了大规模数据采集。

Conclusion: VLA领域的进步需要将**数据基础设施视为一等研究问题**（first-class research problem），而非背景性问题。解决数据层面的根本约束比追求模型架构创新更为关键。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in Vision--Language--Action (VLA) models, a central bottleneck remains underexamined: the data infrastructure that underlies embodied learning. In this survey, we argue that future advances in VLA will depend less on model architecture and more on the co-design of high-fidelity data engines and structured evaluation protocols. To this end, we present a systematic, data-centric analysis of VLA research organized around three pillars: datasets, benchmarks, and data engines. For datasets, we categorize real-world and synthetic corpora along embodiment diversity, modality composition, and action space formulation, revealing a persistent fidelity-cost trade-off that fundamentally constrains large-scale collection. For benchmarks, we analyze task complexity and environment structure jointly, exposing structural gaps in compositional generalization and long-horizon reasoning evaluation that existing protocols fail to address. For data engines, we examine simulation-based, video-reconstruction, and automated task-generation paradigms, identifying their shared limitations in physical grounding and sim-to-real transfer. Synthesizing these analyses, we distill four open challenges: representation alignment, multimodal supervision, reasoning assessment, and scalable data generation. Addressing them, we argue, requires treating data infrastructure as a first-class research problem rather than a background concern.

</details>


### [221] [Equivariant Filter for Radar-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23033)
*Giulio Delama,Jan Michalczyk,Morten Nissov,Martin Scheiber,Alessandro Fornasier,Kostas Alexis,Stephan Weiss*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于等变滤波器(EqF)的雷达-惯性里程计(RIO)方法，通过李群对称性实现导航状态与IMU偏置的几何耦合，并扩展至雷达-IMU外参校准和多状态约束更新。该方法在保持一致性的同时，即使在外部校准初始化极差的情况下也能实现鲁棒状态估计，在无人机实验中达到SOTA精度，并在大校准误差下优于传统EKF方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于EKF的雷达-惯性里程计严重依赖精确的雷达-IMU外参校准，且对扰动敏感，大线性化误差会导致性能下降甚至发散。为解决这些问题，论文引入了等变滤波器框架。

Method: 提出基于李群对称性的等变滤波器(EqF)用于RIO，该对称性几何耦合了导航状态和IMU偏置，并进一步扩展以包含雷达-IMU外参校准和多状态约束更新。等变公式天然保持一致性并增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在两架不同UAV上的真实实验表明，在外参校准正确时，所提EqF-RIO达到SOTA精度；在大校准误差下，传统EKF-RIO失效，而EqF-RIO仍能收敛，表现出了更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EqF-RIO通过等变滤波框架解决了EKF-RIO对外参校准敏感和易发散的问题，在精度和鲁棒性上均优于传统方法，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Radar-Inertial Odometry (RIO) based on the Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) relies on accurate extrinsic calibration between the radar and the Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) and is sensitive to disturbances, as large linearization errors can degrade performance or even cause divergence. To address these limitations, this letter proposes an Equivariant Filter (EqF) for RIO based on a Lie group symmetry that geometrically couples navigation states and IMU biases, extending it to incorporate radar-IMU extrinsic calibration and multi-state constraint updates. This equivariant formulation inherently preserves consistency and enhances robustness, enabling reliable state estimation even under poor or completely wrong initialization of calibration states. Real-world experiments on two different Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) show that the proposed EqF-RIO achieves state-of-the-art accuracy under correct extrinsic calibration and offers improved convergence under large calibration errors, where the conventional EKF-RIO fails. Evaluation code is open-sourced.

</details>


### [222] [Control Barrier Functions Solved with Hierarchical Quadratic Programming for Safe Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23039)
*Rui Luo,Jonas Mariager Jakobsen,Wesley Roozing,Federico Califano,Cheng Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的分层二次规划（HQP）框架，用于物理人机交互中，通过灵活分层设计性能任务和安全任务来平衡安全性与性能，并在真实冗余机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人机交互中安全至关重要，现有CBF方法在处理大量冲突安全任务时存在可行性问题，需要一种更灵活的方式来平衡安全与性能。

Method: 提出基于CBF的分层二次规划（HQP）框架，允许在任意层级设计性能任务（如保持期望的人机交互行为）和安全任务，通过分层松弛解决冲突任务，确保求解可行性。

Result: 在真实冗余机器人上进行了大量实验，验证了该方法在有效性、灵活性和通用性方面的优势。

Conclusion: CBF-HQP框架能够更灵活地平衡物理人机交互中的安全性与性能，为处理多任务冲突提供了有效的分层解决方案。

Abstract: Physical human-robot interaction offers the potential to leverage human intelligence and robot physical capabilities to enable a range of exciting applications, e.g., collaborative robots for rehabilitation. Safety is critical for the successful deployment of this kind of robotic system. In recent years, Control Barrier Function (CBF) has emerged as an effective approach to enforce safety guarantees, which has been widely applied in various applications, from adaptive cruise control to navigation of legged robots. CBFs can be solved in a Quadratic Programming (QP) problem, which can include many CBF-formulated tasks. To manage a large number of safety tasks, a hierarchical CBF has been used to allow hierarchical relaxation of safety tasks to ensure the feasibility of a solution in the presence of conflicting tasks. In this work, we propose to use a CBF-based Hierarchical Quadratic Programming (HQP) framework in physical human-robot interaction to allow us to design both performance tasks (e.g., preserve the desired behavior at the human-robot interaction point) and safety tasks at any level of a hierarchy to balance the safety and the performance in a more flexible way. Extensive experiments were carried out on a real redundant robot to validate the effectiveness, flexibility, and generality of this approach.

</details>


### [223] [A Lightweight Toggleable Adhesion Prototype for Multirotor UAV Landing on Tilting Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23074)
*Teighin Nordholt,Melissa Greeff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级可切换粘附机制，通过电机驱动的螺旋钻与着陆面的钩环材料配合，显著提高了多旋翼无人机在波浪倾斜条件下的着陆成功率（从40%提升至80%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主多旋翼无人机在无人水面舰艇上着陆时，因波浪倾斜、风力扰动和着陆面积有限而导致成功率低、位姿容忍度小，亟需提高着陆可靠性。

Method: 设计了一种电机驱动的螺旋钻机构，与着陆表面的钩环材料（类似魔术贴）配合，实现着陆时的主动粘附与起飞时的可控释放。在改装后的Crazyflie 2.0和固定角度倾斜平台上进行了评估。

Result: 在仅使用简单垂直PID控制器的情况下，该方法在不同倾斜角度（最高43度）下将着陆成功率从平均40%（基线）提升至80%。

Conclusion: 该轻量级可切换粘附机制能有效提高多旋翼无人机在动态海况下的着陆可靠性，且成本低、实现简单。

Abstract: Autonomous multirotor landings on uncrewed surface vessels (USVs) are critical for persistent maritime operations but remain challenging due to wave-induced tilt, wind disturbances, and limited landing area. Many existing approaches exhibit small pose tolerance for reliable landing. This paper presents a lightweight toggleable adhesion mechanism to improve landing reliability. The system uses a motor-driven corkscrew that engages hook-and-loop material on the landing surface, enabling active adhesion during landing and controlled release during takeoff. We evaluate a prototype using a modified Crazyflie 2.0 and a custom tilting platform at fixed angles representative of extreme wave conditions. Using only a simple vertical PID controller, the proposed approach increases landing success from an average of 40% (baseline) to 80% across platform tilts up to 43 degrees using appropriately selected actuation settings.

</details>


### [224] [Breaking Lock-In: Preserving Steerability under Low-Data VLA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23121)
*Suning Huang,Jiaqi Shao,Ke Wang,Qianzhong Chen,Jiankai Sun,Yanjiang Guo,Mac Schwager,Jeannette Bohg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DeLock方法，通过保留视觉基础和测试时对比提示引导，解决VLA策略在低数据微调后出现的“锁定”问题（概念锁定和空间锁定），无需额外监督信号或增强数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在少量演示数据集上微调后，不再响应新指令，仅能执行训练中见过的行为，表现为概念锁定和空间锁定。现有方法依赖额外监督信号或增强数据集，本文旨在仅利用策略内部预训练知识解决该问题。

Method: DeLock方法包含两个关键组件：1) 在微调过程中保留视觉基础能力；2) 在测试时使用对比提示引导（contrastive prompt guidance）来根据新指令调整策略的去噪动态。

Result: 在8项模拟和真实世界评估中，DeLock一致优于强基线方法，与使用大量精选演示数据微调的SOTA通用策略性能相当或更优。

Conclusion: 仅利用策略内部预训练知识即可缓解低数据微调导致的锁定问题，无需额外监督信号或增强数据集，是一种简洁有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Have you ever post-trained a generalist vision-language-action (VLA) policy on a small demonstration dataset, only to find that it stops responding to new instructions and is limited to behaviors observed during post-training? We identify this phenomenon as lock-in: after low-data, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), the policy becomes overly specialized to the post-training data and fails to generalize to novel instructions, manifesting as concept lock-in (fixation on training objects/attributes) and spatial lock-in (fixation on training spatial targets). Many existing remedies introduce additional supervision signals, such as those derived from foundation models or auxiliary objectives, or rely on augmented datasets to recover generalization. In this paper, we show that the policy's internal pre-trained knowledge is sufficient: DeLock mitigates lock-in by preserving visual grounding during post-training and applying test-time contrastive prompt guidance to steer the policy's denoising dynamics according to novel instructions. Across eight simulation and real-world evaluations, DeLock consistently outperforms strong baselines and matches or exceeds the performance of a state-of-the-art generalist policy post-trained with substantially more curated demonstrations.

</details>


### [225] [Cooperative Informative Sensing for Monitoring Dynamic Indoor Environments via Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23179)
*Kanghoon Lee,Matthew M. Sato,Jinnyeong Yang,Seungro Lee,Sujin Lee,Jiachen Li,Kuk-Jin Yoon,Jinkyoo Park,Kincho H. Law,Yoonjin Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体强化学习的框架，用于多机器人协作主动观测人类活动，以直接优化监控精度，在仿真中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖覆盖或访问目标，与监控精度要求弱相关，缺乏直接优化准确性的方案。

Method: 将协作主动观测形式化为部分可观测下的分散控制问题，使用MARL学习策略，架构支持变数量人类和时间依赖。

Result: 在多种室内环境和任务中，该方法一致优于经典覆盖、持续监测和无学习基线，且对观测人数变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于MARL的协作主动观测框架能有效提升监控精度，为多机器人人类监测提供了一种可行的强化学习解决方案。

Abstract: Monitoring human activity in indoor environments is important for applications such as facility management, safety assessment, and space utilization analysis. While mobile robot teams offer the potential to actively improve observation quality, existing multi-robot monitoring and active perception approaches typically rely on coverage or visitation based objectives that are weakly aligned with the accuracy requirements of human-centric monitoring tasks. In this work, we formulate cooperative active observation as a decentralized control problem in which multiple robots adjust their motion to directly optimize monitoring accuracy under partial observability. We propose a learning-based framework for cooperative policies from decentralized observations using multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), supported by an architecture that handles variable numbers of humans and temporal dependencies. Simulation results across diverse indoor environments and monitoring tasks show that the proposed approach consistently outperforms classical coverage, persistent monitoring, and learning-free multi-robot baselines, while remaining robust to changes in the number of observed humans.

</details>


### [226] [BridgeACT: Bridging Human Demonstrations to Robot Actions via Unified Tool-Target Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23249)
*Yifan Han,Jianxiang Liu,Haoyu Zhang,Yuqi Gu,Yunhan Guo,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BridgeACT提出了一种基于affordance的框架，直接从人类视频学习机器人操作技能，无需任何机器人演示数据，通过在场景中定位抓取区域并从人类演示中预测3D运动affordance，再映射为机器人动作实现真实世界部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖机器人数据进行下游适应，要么学到的affordance仅停留在感知层面无法直接支持真实执行。本文旨在直接从人类视频学习可执行的操作策略，无需机器人演示数据。

Method: 将操作分解为"抓取位置"和"运动方式"两个互补问题：首先定位任务相关的affordance区域，然后从人类演示中预测任务条件化的3D运动affordance，最后通过抓取模块和轻量闭环运动控制器映射为机器人动作。复杂任务被表示为affordance操作的组合。

Result: 在真实世界的操作任务上，BridgeACT优于现有基线方法，并能泛化到未见过的物体、场景和视角。

Conclusion: 将affordance作为跨实体中间表征，有效弥合了人类演示与机器人动作之间的鸿沟，实现了无需机器人数据的零样本策略迁移。

Abstract: Learning robot manipulation from human videos is appealing due to the scale and diversity of human demonstrations, but transferring such demonstrations to executable robot behavior remains challenging. Prior work either relies on robot data for downstream adaptation or learns affordance representations that remain at the perception level and do not directly support real-world execution. We present BridgeACT, an affordance-driven framework that learns robotic manipulation directly from human videos without requiring any robot demonstration data. Our key idea is to model affordance as an embodiment-agnostic intermediate representation that bridges human demonstrations and robot actions. BridgeACT decomposes manipulation into two complementary problems: where to grasp and how to move. To this end, BridgeACT first grounds task-relevant affordance regions in the current scene, and then predicts task-conditioned 3D motion affordances from human demonstrations. The resulting affordances are mapped to robot actions through a grasping module and a lightweight closed-loop motion controller, enabling direct deployment on real robots. In addition, we represent complex manipulation tasks as compositions of affordance operations, which allows a unified treatment of diverse tasks and object-to-object interactions. Experiments on real-world manipulation tasks show that BridgeACT outperforms prior baselines and generalizes to unseen objects, scenes, and viewpoints.

</details>


### [227] [Modular Sensory Stream for Integrating Physical Feedback in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23272)
*Jimin Lee,Huiwon Jang,Myungkyu Koo,Jungwoo Park,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoSS提出模块化感知流框架，通过解耦模态流、两阶段训练和辅助预测任务，使VLA模型有效融合触觉、力矩等多模态物理信号，实现协同动作预测性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型通常只依赖单一物理信号，忽视了真实世界交互中多种物理反馈的异构性与互补性，无法充分捕捉丰富的物理交互信息。

Method: 1) 解耦模态流：将异构物理信号通过联合跨模态自注意力机制集成到动作流中；2) 两阶段训练：早期冻结预训练VLA参数，稳定引入新模态；3) 辅助任务：预测未来物理信号，更好地捕捉接触交互动态。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，MoSS成功将触觉和力矩等多种物理信号融入VLA，多种信号协同带来性能增益。

Conclusion: MoSS证明了多模态物理信号融合的有效性，为VLA模型更丰富地感知真实世界交互提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Humans understand and interact with the real world by relying on diverse physical feedback beyond visual perception. Motivated by this, recent approaches attempt to incorporate physical sensory signals into Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs). However, they typically focus on a single type of physical signal, failing to capture the heterogeneous and complementary nature of real-world interactions. In this paper, we propose MoSS, a modular sensory stream framework that adapts VLAs to leverage multiple sensory signals for action prediction. Specifically, we introduce decoupled modality streams that integrate heterogeneous physical signals into the action stream via joint cross-modal self-attention. To enable stable incorporation of new modalities, we adopt a two-stage training scheme that freezes pretrained VLA parameters in the early stage. Furthermore, to better capture contact interaction dynamics, we incorporate an auxiliary task that predicts future physical signals. Through extensive real-world experiments, we demonstrate that MoSS successfully augments VLAs to leverage diverse physical signals (i.e., tactile and torque), integrating multiple signals to achieve synergistic performance gains.

</details>


### [228] [An Efficient Beam Search Algorithm for Active Perception in Mobile Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23327)
*Kaixian Qu,Han Wang,Victor Klemm,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出节点束搜索(NBS)算法和快速探索随机环图(RRAG)构建方法，用于解决自主机器人主动感知中的路径规划问题，在多个任务上相比基线方法提升至少20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动感知方法要么求解计算昂贵的TSP问题，要么采用过度约束的最短路径树，缺乏可扩展且高效的替代方案。

Method: 1) 节点束搜索(NBS)：每节点维护top-B候选路径，更有效探索解空间；2) 预期增益(Expected Gain)指标：引入前沿概念平衡探索与利用；3) 快速探索随机环图(RRAG)：保留完整方向采样并通过后备局部规划器确保连通性。

Result: NBS在低束宽下仍保持强性能；NBS结合RRAG在所有三个代表性主动感知任务中表现最佳，相比SOTA至少提升20%；在真实机器人平台上验证有效。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可扩展且高效的主动感知解决方案，通过NBS和RRAG在计算效率与性能之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Active perception is a fundamental problem in autonomous robotics in which the robot must decide where to move and what to sense in order to obtain the most informative observations for accomplishing its mission. Existing approaches either solve a computationally expensive traveling salesman problem over heuristically selected informative nodes, or adopt a more efficient but overly constrained shortest path tree formulation. To address these limitations, we explore beam search algorithms as scalable alternatives. While the standard beam search provides scalability by preserving the top-B paths at each depth level, it is prone to local optima and exhibits parameter sensitivity. Our first contribution is a node-wise beam search (NBS) algorithm, which maintains top-B candidates per node to enable more effective exploration of the solution space. Systematic benchmarking on graphs shows that NBS consistently outperforms other baselines and maintains strong performance even at low beam widths. As a second contribution, we integrate the concept of frontiers into the path selection criterion, introducing the expected gain metric, which better balances exploration and exploitation compared to existing alternatives. Our third contribution proposes the rapidly-exploring random annulus graph (RRAG), a novel graph construction method that preserves full orientation sampling and ensures connectivity in cluttered environments through a fallback local sampling-based planner. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NBS combined with RRAG achieves the highest performance across all three representative active perception tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art algorithms by at least 20% in one or more tasks. We further validate the approach on real robotic platforms in different scenarios.

</details>


### [229] [Learning from Demonstration with Failure Awareness for Safe Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23360)
*Xianghui Wang,Siwei Cheng,Shanze Wang,Xinming Zhang,Dan Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种失败感知学习框架，将失败数据用于价值估计、成功数据用于策略学习，从而在不破坏策略行为的前提下有效利用碰撞等失败经验，降低机器人导航中的碰撞率并保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习在机器人导航中依赖成功演示数据，对不安全状态的覆盖不足；失败数据（如碰撞）包含关键信息但难以直接用于策略学习，盲目使用反而降低性能。

Method: 提出失败感知框架，明确解耦成功数据与失败数据的角色：失败数据用于塑造危险区域的价值估计，策略学习仅使用成功演示数据；在离线强化学习设置中实现该设计。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，该方法一致地降低了碰撞率，同时保持了任务成功率，并在不同环境和机器人平台上表现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 失败数据可被有效利用来提升导航安全性，关键在于将其与策略学习解耦；该框架为利用失败经验提供了一种实用且有效的方法。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration is widely used for robot navigation, yet it suffers from a fundamental limitation: demonstrations consist predominantly of successful behaviors and provide limited coverage of unsafe states. This limitation leads to poor safety when the robot encounters scenarios beyond the demonstration distribution. Failure experiences, such as collisions, contain essential information about unsafe regions, but remain underutilized. The key difficulty lies in the fact that failure data do not provide valid guidance for action imitation, and their naive incorporation into policy learning often degrades performance. We address this challenge by proposing a failure-aware learning framework that explicitly decouples the roles of success and failure data. In this framework, failure experiences are used to shape value estimation in hazardous regions, while policy learning is restricted to successful demonstrations. This separation enables the effective use of failure data without corrupting policy behavior. We implement this design within an offline reinforcement learning (RL) setting and evaluate it in both simulation and real-world environments. The results show that our framework consistently reduces collision rates while preserving the task success rate, and demonstrate strong generalization across different environments and robot platforms.

</details>


### [230] [Large Language Model based Interactive Decision-Making for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23513)
*Xinwei Dong,Jiyang Li,Jiabin Xie,Yang Yi,Tianshang Jia,Shiyu Fang,Ye Tian,Peng Hang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的交互式决策框架，通过对象-过程方法论（OPM）对多车混行场景进行语义建模，结合LLM解析周围车辆的显式和隐式意图，在安全与效率约束下选择候选策略，并通过蒙特卡洛采样优化轨迹，最终将决策翻译为自然语言通过外部人机界面广播，实现从场景理解到动作再到语言通信的闭环。实验表明该方法在安全性、舒适性和效率上优于传统基线，且决策具有高度类人性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶系统在面对人类驾驶与自动驾驶车辆混行的高冲突场景时，往往默认采取过度保守行为，缺乏主动交互，导致意图误解和决策失败，公众接受度低。

Method: 1) 使用对象-过程方法论（OPM）对多车场景进行语义建模，将低级感知数据抽象为对象、过程和关系；2) 利用LLM解析周围车辆的显式和隐式意图；3) 在安全与效率约束下选择候选策略；4) 通过蒙特卡洛采样生成扰动轨迹并评估；5) 将最终决策由LLM翻译为自然语言，通过eHMI广播。

Result: 在集群驾驶模拟器实验中，该方法在安全性、舒适性和效率指标上均优于传统基线；图灵测试式评估表明决策具有高度的类人性。

Conclusion: 将语义场景抽象与LLM介导的意图推理以及基于语言的eHMI通信相结合，为高密度混行场景下的交互式、可信赖自动驾驶提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: In high-conflict mixed-traffic scenarios involving human-driven and autonomous vehicles, most existing autonomous driving systems default to overly conservative behaviors, lack proactive interaction, and consequently suffer from limited public acceptance. To mitigate intent misunderstandings and decision failures, we present a Large Language Model based interactive decision-making framework that augments scene understanding and intent-aware interaction to jointly improve safety and efficiency. The approach uses Object-Process Methodology to semantically model complex multi-vehicle scenes, abstracting low-level perceptual data into objects, processes, and relations, thereby streamlining reasoning over latent causal structure. Building on this representation, the Large Language Model parses both explicit and implicit intents of surrounding agents and, under jointly enforced safety and efficiency constraints, selects candidate maneuvers. We further generate perturbed trajectory candidates via Monte Carlo sampling and evaluate them to obtain an optimized executable trajectory. To foster transparency and coordination with nearby road users, the final decision is translated by the Large Language Model into concise natural-language messages and broadcast through an external Human-Machine Interface, completing a closed loop from scene understanding to action to language. Experiments in a cluster driving simulator demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms traditional baselines across safety, comfort, and efficiency metrics, while a Turing-test-style evaluation indicates a high degree of human-likeness in decision making. Besides, these results suggest that coupling semantic scene abstraction with Large Language Model mediated intent reasoning and language-based eHMI communication offers a practical pathway toward interactive, trustworthy autonomous driving in dense mixed traffic.

</details>


### [231] [EgoLive: A Large-Scale Egocentric Dataset from Real-World Human Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23570)
*Yihang Li,Xuelong Wei,Jingzhou Luo,Yingjing Xiao,Yibo Bai,Guangyuan Zhou,Teng Zou,Chenguang Gui,Jiajun Wen,He Zhang,Kangliang Chen,Xing Pan,Shuaiyan Liu,Daming Wang,Tao An,Jiayi Li,Shibo Jin,Wanwan Zhang,Tianyu Wang,Boren Wei,Zhixuan Huang,Fangsheng Liu,Ruodai Li,Hui Zhang,Anson Li,Yicheng Gong,Peng Cao,Jiaming Liang,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoLive是一个面向机器人操作学习的大规模、高质量人类第一人称视频数据集，通过头戴式设备在真实无约束场景中采集，涵盖家庭服务、零售等垂直领域数据，旨在为通用机器人模型提供可扩展的高质量数据支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习数据集受限于遥操作等方式的可扩展性和真实部署能力，而人类第一人称视频采集有潜力实现大规模、自然的数据收集。为解决高质量数据稀缺问题，作者构建了EgoLive数据集。

Method: 使用定制头戴式采集设备，在无约束的真实世界场景中收集第一人称视频数据，涵盖家庭服务、零售等垂直工作场景，并提供高精度多模态标注。

Result: EgoLive是当前最大的开源标注第一人称数据集，专注于真实世界任务导向的人类日常操作，具备领先的数据质量和生态效度。

Conclusion: EgoLive为研究社区提供一个可扩展、高质量的数据集，有望加速通用机器人模型的突破，促进机器人系统在真实世界的部署。

Abstract: The advancement of robot learning is currently hindered by the scarcity of large-scale, high-quality datasets. While established data collection methods such as teleoperation and universal manipulation interfaces dominate current datasets, they suffer from inherent limitations in scalability and real-world deployability. Human egocentric video collection, by contrast, has emerged as a promising approach to enable scalable, natural and in-the-wild data collection. As such, we present EgoLive, a large-scale, high-quality egocentric dataset designed explicitly for robot manipulation learning. EgoLive establishes three distinctive technical advantages over existing egocentric datasets: first, it represents the largest open-source annotated egocentric dataset focused on real-world task-oriented human routines to date; second, it delivers leading data quality via a customized head-mounted capture device and comprehensive high-precision multi-modal annotations; third, all data is collected exclusively in unconstrained real-world scenarios and encompasses vertical field human working data, including home service, retail, and other practical work scenarios, providing superior diversity and ecological validity. With the introduction of EgoLive, we aim to provide the research community with a scalable, high-quality dataset that accelerates breakthroughs in generalizable robotic models and facilitates the real-world deployment of robot systems.

</details>


### [232] [PhysCodeBench: Benchmarking Physics-Aware Symbolic Simulation of 3D Scenes via Self-Corrective Multi-Agent Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23580)
*Tianyidan Xie,Peiyu Wang,Yuyi Qian,Yuxuan Wang,Rui Ma,Ying Tai,Song Wu,Qian Wang,Lanjun Wang,Zili Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PhysCodeBench基准测试和SMRF多智能体框架，用于解决物理感知符号模拟问题，SMRF相比最强基线提升31.4分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在通用代码生成上表现出色，但在将物理描述转化为可执行模拟环境时存在语义差距，缺乏专门的评估基准和方法。

Method: 构建包含700个样本的PhysCodeBench基准测试，涵盖力学、流体动力学和软体物理；提出SMRF框架，包含三个专用智能体（模拟生成器、错误纠正器、模拟精炼器）迭代协作并进行领域特定验证。

Result: SMRF达到67.7分，最佳基线为36.3分，提升31.4分；错误纠正对物理感知符号模拟至关重要，多智能体方法显著优于单智能体方法。

Conclusion: 物理感知符号模拟需要专门的错误纠正和多智能体协作策略，SMRF为机器人、具身AI和科学计算等领域提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Physics-aware symbolic simulation of 3D scenes is critical for robotics, embodied AI, and scientific computing, requiring models to understand natural language descriptions of physical phenomena and translate them into executable simulation environments. While large language models (LLMs) excel at general code generation, they struggle with the semantic gap between physical descriptions and simulation implementation. We introduce PhysCodeBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating physics-aware symbolic simulation, comprising 700 manually-crafted diverse samples across mechanics, fluid dynamics, and soft-body physics with expert annotations. Our evaluation framework measures both code executability and physical accuracy through automated and visual assessment. Building on this, we propose a Self-Corrective Multi-Agent Refinement Framework (SMRF) with three specialized agents (simulation generator, error corrector, and simulation refiner) that collaborate iteratively with domain-specific validation to produce physically accurate simulations. SMRF achieves 67.7 points overall performance compared to 36.3 points for the best baseline among evaluated SOTA models, representing a 31.4-point improvement. Our analysis demonstrates that error correction is critical for accurate physics-aware symbolic simulation and that specialized multi-agent approaches significantly outperform single-agent methods across the tested physical domains.

</details>


### [233] [Tube Diffusion Policy: Reactive Visual-Tactile Policy Learning for Contact-rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23609)
*Teng Xue,Alberto Rigo,Bingjian Huang,Jiayi Shen,Zhengtong Xu,Nick Colonnese,Amirhossein H. Memar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Tube Diffusion Policy (TDP)，一种结合扩散模型与管状反馈控制的反应式视觉-触觉策略学习框架，通过在动作块周围形成动作管实现快速自适应反应，在多种接触密集任务中优于现有模仿学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习依赖动作分块（action chunking），难以在执行过程中对未预见的观察做出反应，尤其在接触密集场景中，物理不确定性和高频触觉反馈需要快速反应控制。

Method: 将扩散模型与管状反馈控制结合，学习一个观测条件化的反馈流围绕名义动作块，形成动作管（action tube），实现执行过程中的快速自适应反应。

Result: 在Push-T基准和三个视觉-触觉灵巧操作任务上均优于SOTA模仿学习基线，两个真实世界实验验证了其在接触不确定性和外部干扰下的鲁棒反应能力。

Conclusion: 动作管的逐步骤修正机制显著减少去噪步数，使TDP适用于接触密集操作中的实时高频反馈控制。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation is central to many everyday human activities, requiring continuous adaptation to contact uncertainty and external disturbances through multi-modal perception, particularly vision and tactile feedback. While imitation learning has shown strong potential for learning complex manipulation behaviors, most existing approaches rely on action chunking, which fundamentally limits their ability to react to unforeseen observations during execution. This limitation becomes especially critical in contact-rich scenarios, where physical uncertainty and high-frequency tactile feedback demand rapid, reactive control. To address this challenge, we propose Tube Diffusion Policy (TDP), a novel reactive visual-tactile policy learning framework that bridges diffusion-based imitation learning with tube-based feedback control. By leveraging the expressive power of generative models, TDP learns an observation-conditioned feedback flow around nominal action chunks, forming an action tube that enables fast and adaptive reactions during execution. We evaluate TDP on the widely used Push-T benchmark and three additional challenging visual-tactile dexterous manipulation tasks. Across all benchmarks, TDP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art imitation learning baselines. Two real-world experiments further validate its robust reactivity under contact uncertainty and external disturbances. Moreover, the step-wise correction mechanism enabled by action tube significantly reduces the required denoising steps, making TDP well suited for real-time, high-frequency feedback control in contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [234] [Move-Then-Operate: Behavioral Phasing for Human-Like Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23620)
*Haoming Xu,Lei Lei,Jie Gu,Chu Tang,Jingmin Chen,Ruiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Move-Then-Operate框架，将机器人操作解耦为移动和操作两个阶段，通过双专家策略架构和可学习的阶段选择器，在RoboTwin2基准上达到68.9%的成功率，比基线提升24%，且仅需40%的训练步数即可达到峰值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单一策略模型将粗放移动和精细操作混为一谈，忽略了这两类行为截然不同的动力学特性，导致高精度操作任务表现不佳。作者希望通过显式解耦来引入结构化的归纳偏置。

Method: 1) 双专家策略架构：移动专家负责粗放重定位，操作专家负责接触关键交互；2) 可学习的阶段选择器，根据当前状态动态路由选择哪个专家；3) 基于MLLM的自动阶段标签生成流程，利用末端执行器速度、子任务分解等轻量级上下文线索。

Result: 在RoboTwin2基准上平均成功率达到68.9%，比π₀基线高出24%；与使用10倍数据训练的模型持平或更优；仅需40%的训练步数即可达到峰值性能。

Conclusion: 移动与操作阶段的架构解耦是一种高效且有效的策略，能够显著提升高精度机器人操作任务的性能和训练效率。

Abstract: We present Move-Then-Operate, a Vision language action framework that explicitly decouples robotic manipulation into two distinct behavioral phases: coarse relocation (move) and contact-critical interaction (operate). Unlike monolithic policies that conflate these heterogeneous regimes, our architecture employs a dual-expert policy routed by a learnable phase selector, introducing a structural inductive bias that isolates phase-specific dynamics. Phase labels are automatically generated via an MLLM-based pipeline conditioned on lightweight contextual cues such as end-effector velocity and subtask decomposition to ensure alignment with human motor patterns. Evaluated on the RoboTwin2 benchmark, our method achieves an average success rate of $68.9\%$, outperforming the monolithic $π_0$ baseline by $24\%$. It matches or exceeds models trained on $10\times$ more data and reaches peak performance in $40\%$ fewer training steps, demonstrating that architectural disentanglement of move and operate phases is a highly effective and efficient strategy for mastering high-precision manipulation.

</details>


### [235] [Safe Navigation in Unknown and Cluttered Environments via Direction-Aware Convex Free-Region Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23648)
*Zhicheng Song,Yongjian Li,Kai Chen,Yulin Li,Fan Shi,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将运动方向和机器人几何形状联合纳入凸自由区域生成的导航框架，并通过连续安全轨迹生成实现无碰撞运动，在2D/3D及真实机器人实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有凸自由区域生成方法主要依据周围障碍物几何，在杂乱环境中难以兼顾机器人几何与沿候选运动方向的可通行延伸，且离散采样安全检查无法保证连续无碰撞运动。

Method: 1) 联合候选运动方向和机器人几何生成凸自由区域；2) 区域内基于几何的目标位姿选择与轨迹生成；3) 基于Lipschitz的连续安全认证与局部细化；4) 基于区域图维护增量规划。

Result: 在杂乱2D导航场景中生成更利于下游遍历的自由区域，实现可靠无碰撞导航；3D四足机器人和UAV实验验证了框架的可扩展性和实际可用性。

Conclusion: 联合运动方向与机器人几何的凸区域生成策略能有效提升狭窄通道中的导航性能，结合连续安全认证可实现更可靠的自主导航。

Abstract: Convex free regions provide a structured and optimization-friendly representation of collision-free space for robot navigation in unknown and cluttered environments. However, existing methods typically enlarge local collision-free regions mainly according to surrounding obstacle geometry. In cluttered environments, such strategies may fail to generate regions that both accommodate robot geometry and preserve traversable extension along candidate motion directions, thereby limiting downstream traversal, especially in narrow passages. Even when such a region is available, safe motion generation remains challenging, because safety checking at discretized trajectory samples does not guarantee continuously collision-free motion when robot geometry is modeled explicitly. To address these issues, we propose a navigation framework that jointly incorporates candidate motion directions and robot geometry into convex free-region generation, and achieves continuously collision-free motion through continuous-safe trajectory generation. Within each region, the framework performs geometry-aware target pose selection and trajectory generation, together with Lipschitz-based continuous safety certification and local refinement. The resulting free regions and candidate motions are maintained in a region-based graph to support incremental planning. Quantitative results in cluttered 2D navigation scenarios show that the proposed method generates free regions better aligned with downstream traversal and enables reliable collision-free navigation, while additional 3D and real-world experiments on a quadrupedal robot and a UAV demonstrate the extensibility and practical applicability of the framework. The open-source project can be found at https://github.com/ZhichengSong6/FRGraph.

</details>


### [236] [Decentralized Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Collaborative Exploration for Indoor and Outdoor 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23693)
*Yuxiang Li,Kun Chen,Jiancheng Wang,Shihao Fang,Haoyao Chen,Yunhui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向异构多机器人系统的去中心化协同探索框架，通过改进的超体素分割、能力建模和遗传算法求解HMDMTSP，实现室内外3D环境的高效自主探索，在探索效率和通信开销上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多机器人系统在复杂环境中具有显著适应性，但如何充分利用机器人潜能实现高效协作仍是核心挑战。

Method: 1) 构建融合地形与观测指标的基础感知地图，通过改进超体素分割简化地图结构；2) 对异构机器人的遍历和观测能力建模，评估不完整超体素的任务视点需求；3) 将视点聚类分配建模为异构多仓库多旅行商问题(HMDMTSP)，设计改进遗传算法求解；4) 消除冗余视点并解决运动路径冲突。

Result: 在杂乱的室内外环境中的仿真和实地实验表明，该方法在探索效率和通信节省方面均优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效协调异构机器人间的探索任务，实现高效协同探索。

Abstract: Heterogeneous multi-robot systems feature significant adaptability for complex environments. However, effective collaboration that fully exploits the robots' potential remains a core challenge. This paper proposes a decentralized collaborative framework for heterogeneous multi-robot systems to autonomously explore indoor and outdoor 3D environments. First, a basic perception map that integrates terrain and observation metrics is designed. Improved supervoxel segmentation is developed to simplify the map structure and form a high-level representation that supports lightweight communication. Second, the traversal and observation capabilities of heterogeneous robots are modeled to evaluate the requirements of task views derived from incomplete supervoxels. These task views are grouped by requirements and clustered to streamline assignment. Subsequently, the view-cluster assignment is formulated as a heterogeneous multi-depot multi-traveling salesman problem (HMDMTSP) that incorporates constraints between view-cluster requirements and robot capabilities. An improved genetic algorithm is developed to efficiently solve this problem while ensuring global consistency. Based on the assignments, redundant views within clusters are eliminated to refine exploration routes. Finally, conflicts between robots' motion paths are resolved. Simulations and field experiments in cluttered indoor and outdoor environments demonstrate that our approach effectively coordinates exploration tasks among heterogeneous robots, achieving superior exploration efficiency and communication savings compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [237] [Real-Time Non-Contact Force Compensation for Wrist-Mounted Force/Torque Sensors in Haptic-Enabled Robotic Surgery Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23696)
*Walid Shaker,Mustafa Suphi Erden*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人手术触觉反馈成本高的问题，提出了一种基于递归最小二乘法(RLS)的实时补偿方法，用于腕部安装的力/扭矩传感器，实现了95%以上的非接触力补偿和91%以上的扭矩补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助手术中触觉反馈一直缺失，现有商用系统集成触觉技术成本过高，限制了训练和研究用途的可及性。

Method: 在之前开发的低成本手术训练平台RoboScope基础上，加装腕部安装的力/扭矩传感器，采用基于递归最小二乘法(RLS)的实时补偿方法，消除重力、传感器偏差、安装偏移等非接触力干扰。

Result: 非接触力补偿误差降低超过95%，非接触扭矩补偿误差降低超过91%，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法无需数据集收集和频繁重新校准，能适应变化的操作条件，为机器人手术训练和研究提供可靠的触觉反馈。

Abstract: Haptic feedback has been a long-missed feature in robotic-assisted surgery, one that would allow surgeons to perceive tissue properties and apply controlled forces during delicate procedures. Although commercial robotic systems have begun to integrate haptic technologies, their high costs limit accessibility for training and research purposes. To address this gap, we extend our previously developed low-cost robotic surgery training setup, RoboScope, by incorporating a wrist-mounted force/torque (F/T) sensor for haptic feedback training. Wrist-mounted sensing avoids many challenges associated with tip-mounted sensors but introduces additional non-contact forces, such as gravity, sensor bias, installation offsets, and associated torques, which compromise measurement accuracy. In this paper, we propose a robust real-time compensation method based on recursive least squares (RLS). This method eliminates the need for dataset collection and frequent recalibration while adapting to changing operating conditions. Experimental validation demonstrates that the proposed approach achieves over 95% error reduction in non-contact force compensation and more than 91% in non-contact torque compensation, significantly outperforming existing methods. These results highlight the potential of our approach for providing reliable haptic feedback in robotic surgery training and research.

</details>


### [238] [QuietWalk: Physics-Informed Reinforcement Learning for Ground Reaction Force-Aware Humanoid Locomotion Under Diverse Footwear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23702)
*Hanze Hu,Luying Feng,Silu Chen,Tianjiang Zheng,Dexin Jiang,Wei Chen,Chi Zhang,Guilin Yang,Yaochu Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QuietWalk提出了一种物理信息驱动的强化学习框架，通过逆动力学约束的PINN估计足底垂直地面反作用力（GRF），并将其作为惩罚项融入训练，实现人形机器人在多种 footwear 条件下的低噪声行走，在真实机器人数据集上GRF预测误差降低82%-86%，噪声降低最高7.17 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在人类中心环境下运行时，足-地碰撞产生的振动和噪声会降低用户体验，重复冲击还会加速硬件磨损。现有方法依赖运动学代理目标或易损力传感器，且不同鞋履引入的接触动力学变化导致策略泛化困难。

Method: 采用逆动力学约束的物理信息神经网络（PINN）从本体感知信号估计每只脚垂直GRF，将冻结的预测器集成到RL训练循环中，以惩罚预测的冲击力，无需部署力传感器。

Result: 在留出真实机器人数据集上，逆动力学一致性约束使垂直GRF预测误差降低82%-86%，决定系数从0.39/0.67提升至0.99/0.99。在1.2 m/s（赤脚）硬件实验中，平均A加权噪声降低7.17 dB，峰值噪声降低4.98 dB。跨鞋履实验验证了对接触变化的鲁棒适应。

Conclusion: QuietWalk通过物理约束的GRF预测与RL训练结合，有效实现了人形机器人在多变鞋履条件下的低噪声行走，且无需部署力传感器，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Humanoid robots operating in human-centered environments (e.g., homes, hospitals, and offices) must mitigate foot--ground impact transients, as impact-induced vibration and noise degrade user experience and repeated impacts accelerate hardware wear. However, existing low-noise locomotion training often relies on kinematic proxy objectives or fragile force sensors, and footwear-induced changes in contact dynamics introduce distribution shifts that hinder policy generalization.We present QuietWalk, a physics-informed reinforcement learning framework for ground-reaction-force-aware humanoid locomotion under diverse footwear conditions. QuietWalk employs an inverse-dynamics-constrained physics-informed neural network (PINN) to estimate per-foot vertical ground reaction forces (GRFs) from proprioceptive signals, and integrates the frozen predictor into the RL training loop to penalize predicted impact forces without requiring force sensors at deployment.On a held-out real-robot dataset, enforcing inverse-dynamics consistency reduces vertical GRF prediction errors by 82%-86% compared with a purely supervised predictor and improves the coefficient of determination from 0.39/0.67 to 0.99/0.99 for the left/right feet. On hardware at 1.2 m/s (barefoot; averaged over four floor materials), QuietWalk reduces mean A-weighted noise level by 7.17 dB and peak noise level by 4.98 dB under a consistent recording setup. Cross-footwear experiments (barefoot, skate shoes, athletic sneakers, and high heels) across multiple surfaces further demonstrate robust adaptation to footwear-induced contact variations.

</details>


### [239] [Unleashing the Agility of Wheeled-Legged Robots for High-Dynamic Reflexive Obstacle Evasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23761)
*Yongen Zhao,Zihao Xu,Wenzhi Lu,Zhen Chu,Ce Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AWARE框架，一种用于轮腿机器人的分层强化学习方法，实现高速动态避障，具有多种涌现的规避行为（前冲、侧闪等），在仿真和实物平台上验证了其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮腿机器人虽兼具轮式和腿式优势，但面对快速移动障碍物时，受限于混合形态、模态耦合和非完整约束，难以实现高动态反射式规避，现有方法不足。

Method: 提出AWARE（Adaptive Wheeled-Legged Avoidance and Reflexive Evasion），一种分层强化学习框架，使机器人自主涌现多种规避步态和行为策略。

Result: 在Isaac Lab仿真和M20实物平台上，AWARE在多种动态场景下实现了鲁棒且敏捷的避障，展现出前冲、侧闪等行为各异的规避策略。

Conclusion: AWARE在轮腿机器人高动态避障任务上具有实际有效性，同时揭示了轮腿机器人内在的反射式敏捷能力。

Abstract: Wheeled-legged robots combine the energy efficiency of wheeled locomotion with the terrain adaptability of legged systems, making them promising platforms for agile mobility in complex and dynamic environments. However, enabling high-dynamic reflexive evasion against fast-moving obstacles remains challenging due to the hybrid morphology, mode coupling, and non-holonomic constraints of such platforms. In this work, we propose AWARE, Adaptive Wheeled-Legged Avoidance and Reflexive Evasion, a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework for high-dynamic obstacle avoidance in wheeled-legged robots. The proposed system naturally exhibits diverse emergent gaits and evasive behaviors, including forward lunge and lateral dodge, thereby leveraging the robot's hybrid morphology to enhance agility under highly dynamic threats. Extensive experiments in Isaac Lab simulation and real-world deployment on the M20 platform across diverse dynamic scenarios demonstrate that AWARE achieves robust and agile obstacle avoidance while revealing behaviorally distinct evasive strategies. These results highlight both the practical effectiveness of AWARE and the intrinsic reflexive agility of wheeled-legged robots.

</details>


### [240] [Vision-Language-Action Safety: Threats, Challenges, Evaluations, and Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23775)
*Qi Li,Bo Yin,Weiqi Huang,Ruhao Liu,Bojun Zou,Runpeng Yu,Jingwen Ye,Weihao Yu,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的安全问题，从攻击时机（训练时/推理时）和防御时机两个并行维度组织文献，涵盖攻击、防御、评估和部署四大视角，并指出了五个关键开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型正成为具身智能的统一基础，但其具身特性带来了不可逆物理后果、多模态攻击面、实时延迟约束、长程误差传播和数据供应链脆弱性等全新安全挑战，而现有文献分散在多个不同领域，缺乏统一视角。

Method: 本文采用双轴时序框架（攻击时机×防御时机）组织综述，从四个视角（攻击、防御、评估、部署）系统梳理文献，涵盖训练时威胁（数据投毒、后门攻击）和推理时攻击（对抗补丁、跨模态扰动、语义越狱、冻结攻击），以及对应的训练时和运行时防御策略。

Result: 识别了VLA安全与文本LLM安全及传统机器人安全的差异，系统总结了各类攻击与防御的现状，分析了现有基准与评估指标，并概括了六大部署领域的安全挑战。

Conclusion: 提出了五个开放问题：具身轨迹的认证鲁棒性、物理可实现防御、安全感知训练、统一运行时安全架构、标准化评估体系。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are emerging as a unified substrate for embodied intelligence. This shift raises a new class of safety challenges, stemming from the embodied nature of VLA systems, including irreversible physical consequences, a multimodal attack surface across vision, language, and state, real-time latency constraints on defense, error propagation over long-horizon trajectories, and vulnerabilities in the data supply chain. Yet the literature remains fragmented across robotic learning, adversarial machine learning, AI alignment, and autonomous systems safety. This survey provides a unified and up-to-date overview of safety in Vision-Language-Action models. We organize the field along two parallel timing axes, attack timing (training-time vs. inference-time and defense timing (training-time vs. inference-time, linking each class of threat to the stage at which it can be mitigated. We first define the scope of VLA safety, distinguishing it from text-only LLM safety and classical robotic safety, and review the foundations of VLA models, including architectures, training paradigms, and inference mechanisms. We then examine the literature through four lenses: Attacks, Defenses, Evaluation, and Deployment. We survey training-time threats such as data poisoning and backdoors, as well as inference-time attacks including adversarial patches, cross-modal perturbations, semantic jailbreaks, and freezing attacks. We review training-time and runtime defenses, analyze existing benchmarks and metrics, and discuss safety challenges across six deployment domains. Finally, we highlight key open problems, including certified robustness for embodied trajectories, physically realizable defenses, safety-aware training, unified runtime safety architectures, and standardized evaluation.

</details>


### [241] [Cooptimizing Safety and Performance Using Safety Value-Constrained Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23863)
*Hao Wang,Nam Nguyen,Armand Jordana,Ludovic Righetti,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合安全值函数作为终端约束的MPC方法，在有限规划horizon之外也能保证系统的持续安全性，通过仿真和机械臂实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MPC虽然能在有限时间窗口内实现约束最优控制，但无法保证超出规划时域后的安全性。传统终端集构造依赖局部线性化或保守近似，限制了安全保证的表达能力。

Method: 在MPC中引入基于可达性分析的安全值函数作为终端约束，强制规划终点位于控制不变的安全集内，从而确保持续安全性。

Result: 在Flexiv Rizon 10s机械臂上的仿真与硬件实验中，该方法相比标准状态约束MPC和反应式安全滤波，在约束满足和鲁棒性方面均有提升，同时保持了竞争性的任务性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的安全值函数终端约束MPC能够在保持高性能的同时实现可证明的持续安全，且比传统方法更少保守性。

Abstract: Autonomous systems are increasingly deployed in real-world environments, where they must achieve high performance while maintaining safety under state and input constraints. Although Model Predictive Control (MPC) provides a principled framework for constrained optimal control, guaranteeing safety beyond its finite planning horizon remains a fundamental challenge. In this work, we augment MPC with a safety value function-based terminal constraint that enforces membership in a control-invariant safe set at the end of each planning horizon. This formulation enables real-time synthesis of trajectories that are both high-performing and provably safe. We show that, under an exact safety value function and a feasible initialization, the proposed MPC scheme is recursively feasible, thereby ensuring persistent safety. In contrast to traditional terminal set constructions that rely on local linearizations or conservative approximations, our approach incorporates a reachability-based safety value function for terminal constraints, yielding less conservative and more expressive safety guarantees. We validate the proposed framework through simulation and hardware experiments on a Flexiv Rizon 10s manipulator. Results demonstrate improved constraint satisfaction and robustness compared to standard state-constrained MPC and reactive safety filtering, while maintaining competitive task performance. The full implementation and experiments are available on the project website.

</details>


### [242] [Multi-Robot Motions in Milliseconds: Vector-Accelerated Primitives for Sampling-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23960)
*James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了VAMP框架至多机器人运动规划，开发了两个向量加速原语（多机器人MotVal和FFC），利用SIMD并行性实现了超过1100倍的验证加速和850倍以上的规划加速，能在毫秒级求解多机器人规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将VAMP框架从单机器人扩展到多机器人运动规划（MRMP）领域，以解决多机器人场景下的计算效率瓶颈问题。

Method: 开发了两个向量加速原语：多机器人MotionValidation (MotVal) 和 FindFirstConflict (FFC)，利用SIMD并行技术加速多机器人运动验证和冲突检测；并修改了多种代表性MRMP算法以集成这些原语。

Result: 多机器人运动验证加速比超过1100倍；多种算法规划加速比超过850倍；部分实例能在毫秒级求解包含机械臂、刚体及异构团队的多机器人规划问题。

Conclusion: 向量加速方法可显著提升多机器人运动规划的计算效率，具有广泛适用性和实用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend the recent Vector-Accelerated Motion Planning (VAMP) framework to multi-robot motion planning (MRMP). We develop two vector-accelerated primitives, multi-robot MotionValidation (MotVal) and FindFirstConflict (FFC), which exploit SIMD parallelism within the multi-robot domain. On pure multi-robot motion validation tests, this achieves over 1100X speedup in validation time. Additionally, we modify a representative set of MRMP algorithms to use these new primitives. The relative speedup for each algorithm is studied on scenarios with manipulator, rigid body, and heterogeneous teams with some instances producing multi-robot solutions in the order of milliseconds and, in many cases, shows planning time speedups of over 850X.

</details>


### [243] [Designing Robots to Support Parent-Child Connections: Opportunities Through Robot-Mediated Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23976)
*Michael F Xu,Bengisu Cagiltay,Yaxin Hu,Anjun Zhu,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过两项家庭研究（6个家庭的居家技术探针+20个家庭的实验室研究），探索了机器人如何促进亲子连接，重点分析了机器人的行为策略（被动/反应/主动）和通讯模式（同步/异步）两个设计维度对亲子互动的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科技发展往往取代人际互动，作者希望探索机器人如何成为促进家庭连接的沟通工具，而非替代亲子互动。

Method: 两项研究：1）6个家庭的居家技术探针探索设计空间；2）20个家庭的实验室研究，系统考察机器人行为策略（被动/反应/主动）和通讯模式（同步/异步）两个维度。

Result: 揭示了家长和儿童如何挪用机器人介导的交流，以及在主动权、时机、隐私方面的张力，以及他们对支持日常连接的机会想象。

Conclusion: 机器人可以成为促进家庭连接的新工具，但设计需谨慎平衡用户对主动权、时机和隐私的需求。

Abstract: The sense of family connectedness may support positive outcomes including individual well-being, resilience, and healthy family functioning. However, as technologies advance, they often replace human-human interactions instead of nurturing them. In this work, we investigate how robot-facilitated communication tools might instead create new opportunities for family connection. We conducted two studies with families with children aged 5-12. We first explored the design space through in-home technology probe sessions with six families. These probes inspired us to explore two key interaction design dimensions: the robot's behavior strategy (passive, reactive, proactive) and the mode of communication (synchronous, asynchronous). We then conducted a laboratory study with 20 families to examine how the two dimensions shaped parent-child interaction and connection. Our findings characterize how parents and children appropriated robot-mediated exchanges, the tensions they experienced around initiative, timing, and privacy, and the opportunities they envisioned for supporting everyday connectedness.

</details>


### [244] [Supporting Family-School Partnerships with Robot-Facilitated Home-Based Activities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23978)
*Michael F Xu,Qiyao Yang,Heather Kirkorian,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索将社交机器人集成到家庭环境中，以支持家校合作。通过访谈和协同设计开发机器人系统，并在10个家庭中进行为期一周的居家评估。结果显示机器人能融入家庭生活，家长引导风格影响使用方式，同时揭示了机器人的帮助性和挑战。提供了家庭-机器人交互的设计启示和伦理隐私考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家校合作对儿童发展至关重要，但家庭常面临时间限制、沟通碎片化以及缺乏有意义的参与机会等障碍。为促进更广泛的家校合作，研究者探索将社交机器人引入家庭场景以支持家庭活动。

Method: 通过访谈和协同设计环节，与家长和儿童共同设计和开发了一套机器人系统，支持家庭关于学校话题的沟通。在10个家庭中进行了为期一周的居家实地研究评估。

Result: 机器人能够融入家庭日常生活；家长的不同引导风格影响了机器人使用方式；家庭既感知到机器人的帮助性，也面临一些挑战。

Conclusion: 提供了实证洞见，提出了模块化系统，给出了家庭-儿童-机器人交互的设计启示，拓展了技术支撑家校合作的设计空间，并讨论了伦理和隐私考量。

Abstract: Family-school partnerships (FSP) are critical to children's development, yet families often face barriers such as time constraints, fragmented communication, and limited opportunities for meaningful engagement. As a step toward facilitating broader family-school partnerships, we explore a novel approach that integrates a social robot into family settings, specifically supporting home-based activities. Through interviews and co-design sessions, we designed and developed a robotic system informed by both parents and children, that supported, among other interactions, family communication about school topics. We evaluated the robot in a week-long, in-home study with 10 families. Our findings show how families integrated the robot into daily life, how parental facilitation styles shaped use, and how families perceived both the helpfulness and challenges of the robot. We contribute empirical insights, a modular system, and design implications for family- and child-robot interactions. We discuss ethical and privacy considerations, and broaden the design space for technologies supporting family-school partnerships.

</details>


### [245] [Betting for Sim-to-Real Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24018)
*Zaid Mahboob,Yujia Chen,Bowen Weng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从"赌博"视角研究机器人在物理实验受限下的性能评估问题，提出一种基于赌注机制的估计方法，理论上优于传统蒙特卡洛估计，并通过合成数据与仿真实验验证了其在sim-to-real场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理机器人实验成本高、耗时长且受安全限制，导致真实世界试验数据稀缺。现有方法（如重要性采样、预测驱动推断）虽能减少方差或校正偏差，但仍有改进空间。本文希望探索一种新的互补视角来更高效地评估机器人性能。

Method: 将性能评估问题转化为"赌注"问题，理论推导赌注机制能优于蒙特卡洛估计的条件，设计实用的近似赌注构造方法，并提供诊断策略是否正常工作的决策规则。采用合成数据与跨保真度计算仿真器进行验证。

Result: 理论证明赌注机制在特定条件下可准确高效地估计机器人性能，超越蒙特卡洛估计。合成数据与仿真实验验证了方法有效性。一个有意思的案例是用合成分布推断真实机械臂的抓取精度，展示了赌博视角下sim-to-real迁移的自然可行性。

Conclusion: 赌博视角为机器人性能评估提供了一种新颖且有理论保障的方法，能够在物理实验极度受限时仍获得可靠估计，可作为现有方差缩减或偏差校正方法的补充。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of robot performance evaluation, focusing on how to obtain accurate and efficient estimates of real-world behavior under severe constraints on physical experimentation. Such estimates are essential for benchmarking algorithms, comparing design alternatives, validating controllers, and supporting certification or regulatory decision-making, yet real-world testing with physical robots is often expensive, time-consuming, and safety-limited. To mitigate the scarcity of real-world trials, sim-to-real methodologies are commonly employed, using low-cost simulators to inform, supplement, or prioritize physical experiments. Departing from (and complementary to) existing approaches in variance reduction (e.g., importance-sampling variants) or bias-correction (e.g., through prediction-powered inference or learned control variates), we examine this performance-evaluation problem through the lens of betting. We establish theoretical conditions under which a betting mechanism can yield accurate and efficient estimates (provably outperforming the Monte Carlo estimator) and we characterize how such bets should be constructed. We further develop theoretically grounded yet practically implementable approximations of the ideal bet, and we provide concrete decision rules that diagnose when these approximate betting strategies are working as intended. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods using both synthetic examples and cross-fidelity computational simulators. Notably, we also showcase an illustrative case in which a group of synthetic distributions are used to infer the real-world pick-and-place accuracy of a robotic manipulator, a seemingly unconventional sim-to-real transfer that becomes natural and feasible under the proposed betting perspective. Programs for reproducing empirical results are available at https://github.com/ISUSAIL/Bet4Sim2Real.

</details>


### [246] [Event-based SLAM Benchmark for High-Speed Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24033)
*Sheng Zhong,Junkai Niu,Guillermo Gallego,Kaizhen Sun,Yang Yi,Zhiqiang Miao,Dewen Hu,Yaonan Wang,Davide Scaramuzza,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了现有事件相机视觉里程计/视觉-惯性里程计方法的局限性，指出现有数据集和评估指标的不足，并提出了一个名为EvSLAM的基准测试框架，该框架涵盖多样化的数据采集平台、极端光照场景和具有挑战性的运动模式，并引入新的评估指标，以公平评估事件相机在高速机动场景下的状态估计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机方法虽能缓解高速运动模糊，但存在诸多限制：有的依赖局部地图持续可见，有的仅适用于纯旋转运动，无法推广到六自由度任意激烈机动场景。作者希望通过构建全面的基准框架，系统评估现有方法的真实能力和局限性。

Method: 作者首先对现有事件相机VO/VIO方法进行深入分析，指出现有数据集存在缺陷；然后构建EvSLAM基准评估框架，包含多样化的数据采集平台、极端光照场景、清晰定义的移动机器人高速机动模式，并设计了新的评估指标来公平评估事件相机解决方案的极限。

Result: 对现有最先进方法进行基准测试，揭示了最优架构设计方向以及当前方法面临的持续挑战。

Conclusion: 事件相机在任意激烈机动下的状态估计问题尚未完全解决；EvSLAM框架为未来研究提供了更公平、全面的评估标准。

Abstract: Event-based cameras are bio-inspired sensors with pixels that independently and asynchronously respond to brightness changes at microsecond resolution, offering the potential to handle visual tasks in high-speed maneuvering scenarios. Existing event-based approaches, although successful in mitigating motion blur caused by high-speed maneuvers, suffer from many limitations. Some of them highlight a success of pose tracking for a fronto-parallel fast shaking camera closed to the structure, while others assume pure (optionally aggressive) three-degree-of-freedom rotations. The former requires persistent local map visibility within the field of view (FOV), whereas the latter fails to generalize to six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) motions where both linear and angular velocities may be large. Consequently, current successes do not fully demonstrate that event-based state estimation under arbitrary aggressive maneuvers is a fully solved problem. To quantitatively assess the extent to which the potential of event cameras has been unlocked, we conduct a thorough analysis of state-of-the-art (SOTA) event-based visual odometry (VO)/visual-inertial odometry (VIO) methods and report shortcomings in current public datasets. Furthermore, we introduce a benchmarking framework for event-based state estimation, called EvSLAM, characterized by sufficient variation in data collection platforms, diverse extreme lighting scenarios, and a wide scope of challenging motion patterns under a clear and rigorous definition of high-speed maneuvers for mobile robots, along with a novel evaluation metric designed to fairly assess the operational limits of event-based solutions. This framework benchmarks state-of-the-art methods, yielding insights into optimal architectures and persistent challenges.

</details>


### [247] [Trajectory Planning for an Articulated Commercial Vehicle using Model Predictive Contouring Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24064)
*A. J. Aertssen,R. G. M. Huisman,I. J. M. Besselink,J. Elfring,M. J. G. van de Molengraft*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MPCC的 articulated 商用车（牵引车-挂车）轨迹规划方法，通过扩展MPCC实现场景相关锚点加权和显式道路边界约束，成功完成了正反向物流场景的导航，并在实车测试中验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPCC方法主要适用于乘用车，但牵引车-挂车体积大、挂车轨迹与牵引车不同、倒车不稳定易发生折叠，且实际驾驶中需要根据场景对不同锚点（牵引车/挂车位置）进行优先级调整，现有方法无法满足这些需求。

Method: 扩展MPCC框架，引入场景相关锚点加权机制和显式道路边界约束（覆盖前/后牵引车轴及挂车轴），确保所有轮轴保持在可行驶区域内。

Result: 在代表性物流场景中成功实现正反向导航；分析了优化参数对轨迹的影响；全尺寸原型车初步测试验证了实际可行性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了大型铰接车辆在复杂场景下的轨迹规划问题，尤其适用于倒车和空间受限作业（如对接、充电停车），为商用车辆自动化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a trajectory planning method for articulated commercial vehicles, specifically tractor-semitrailers, based on Model Predictive Contouring Control (MPCC). Although MPCC has proven effective for passenger cars, it is generally ill-suited for tractor-semitrailers. These vehicles are significantly larger, the semitrailer follows a different path than the tractor, and reversing maneuvers are unstable and prone to jackknifing. Furthermore, practical driving scenarios often require scenario-dependent prioritization of different vehicle `anchor points', e.g., prioritizing the semitrailer position during docking or the tractor position when parking to charge. Therefore, we extend MPCC to enable scenario-dependent weighting of these anchor points and incorporate explicit road-boundary constraints for the front and rear tractor axles and the semitrailer axle, thereby ensuring that all considered wheels remain within the drivable area. The simulation results demonstrate the successful navigation of a representative logistic scenario in both forward and reverse direction. Furthermore, the influence of the optimization parameters on the trajectories is analyzed, providing insights into controlling the vehicle behavior. Finally, first tests using a full-scale prototype vehicle show the practical applicability of the approach.

</details>


### [248] [AsyncShield: A Plug-and-Play Edge Adapter for Asynchronous Cloud-based VLA Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24086)
*Kai Yang,Zedong Chu,Yingnan Guo,Zhengbo Wang,Shichao Xie,Yanfen Shen,Xiaolong Wu,Xing Li,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AsyncShield，一种即插即用的异步控制框架，通过物理白盒空间映射解决云端VLA模型部署时因网络延迟导致的时空错位问题，在不微调云端模型的情况下提升导航成功率和物理安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽具备零样本泛化能力，但其参数量大需云端部署，而云部署引入的网络抖动和推理延迟导致移动导航中连续位移下的时空错位，使得过时的意图在当前帧中空间上不正确，进而引发碰撞。

Method: 1) 抛弃传统黑盒时间序列预测，采用确定性物理白盒空间映射，通过时间位姿缓冲区和运动学变换将时间滞后转化为空间位姿偏移；2) 将边缘适配建模为带约束的马可夫决策过程(CMDP)，用PPO-Lagrangian算法求解；3) 通过标准化通用子目标接口、域随机化和碰撞半径膨胀实现即插即用。

Result: 仿真和真实实验表明，在不微调任何云端基础模型的情况下，该框架展现出零样本和鲁棒的泛化能力，有效提升了异步导航的成功率和物理安全性。

Conclusion: AsyncShield通过物理白盒空间映射替代黑盒预测，结合强化学习适配器平衡意图恢复保真度与物理安全，为云端VLA模型在机器人导航中的异步部署提供了一种轻量级、即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have been demonstrated possessing strong zero-shot generalization for robot control, their massive parameter sizes typically necessitate cloud-based deployment. However, cloud deployment introduces network jitter and inference latency, which can induce severe spatiotemporal misalignment in mobile navigation under continuous displacement, so that the stale intents expressed in past ego frames may become spatially incorrect in the current frame and lead to collisions. To address this issue, we propose AsyncShield, a plug-and-play asynchronous control framework. AsyncShield discards traditional black-box time-series prediction in favor of a deterministic physical white-box spatial mapping. By maintaining a temporal pose buffer and utilizing kinematic transformations, the system accurately converts temporal lag into spatial pose offsets to restore the VLA's original geometric intent. To balance intent restoration fidelity and physical safety, the edge adaptation is formulated as a constrained Markov decision process (CMDP). Solved via the PPO-Lagrangian algorithm, a reinforcement learning adapter dynamically trades off between tracking the VLA intent and responding to high-frequency LiDAR obstacle avoidance hard constraints. Furthermore, benefiting from a standardized universal sub-goal interface, domain randomization, and perception-level adaptation via Collision Radius Inflation, AsyncShield operates as a lightweight, plug-and-play module. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that, without fine-tuning any cloud-based foundation models, the framework exhibits zero-shot and robust generalization capabilities, effectively improving the success rate and physical safety of asynchronous navigation.

</details>


### [249] [$M^2$-VLA: Boosting Vision-Language Models for Generalizable Manipulation via Layer Mixture and Meta-Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24182)
*Siyao Xiao,Yuhong Zhang,Zhifang Liu,Zihan Gao,Jingye Zhang,Sinwai Choo,Dake Zhong,Mengzhe Wang,Xiao Lin,Xianfeng Zhou,Jia Jia,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出M²-VLA框架，通过混合层策略(MoL)和元技能模块(MSM)让通用VLM直接作为机器人操作骨干，避免端到端微调导致的泛化能力丧失和灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖端到端微调，这会损害VLM固有的泛化能力并导致灾难性遗忘。作者希望让通用VLM直接作为机器人操作的骨干网络，同时弥合高层语义理解与精确控制需求之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出Mixture of Layers (MoL)策略，从密集语义特征中选择性提取任务关键信息；提出Meta Skill Module (MSM)集成强归纳偏置，促进在有限模型容量下的高效轨迹学习。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验验证了方法的有效性，泛化和消融研究证明了架构的零样本能力及各关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: M²-VLA成功展示了通用VLM可直接作为机器人操作的强大骨干，无需依赖端到端微调，为机器人操作提供了一种保留泛化能力的新范式。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models predominantly rely on end-to-end fine-tuning. While effective, this paradigm compromises the inherent generalization capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and incurs catastrophic forgetting. To address these limitations, we propose $M^2$-VLA, which demonstrates that a generalized VLM is able to serve as a powerful backbone for robotic manipulation directly. However, it remains a key challenge to bridge the gap between the high-level semantic understanding of VLMs and the precise requirements of robotic control. To overcome this, we introduce the Mixture of Layers (MoL) strategy that selectively extracts task-critical information from dense semantic features. Furthermore, to facilitate efficient trajectory learning under constrained model capacity, we propose a Meta Skill Module (MSM) that integrates strong inductive biases. Extensive experiments in both simulated and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Furthermore, generalization and ablation studies validate the architecture's zero-shot capabilities and confirm the contribution of each key component. Our code and pre-trained models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [250] [Generalizable Friction Coefficient Estimation via Material Embedding and Proxy Interaction Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24188)
*Zhendong Wang,Huamin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于代理材料的摩擦系数预测框架，通过学习材料嵌入和融合函数，仅需少量代理测量即可近似任意材料对的摩擦系数，大幅减少实验成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意材料对之间的摩擦系数精确估计对机器人、数字制造和物理仿真至关重要，但穷举测试成本随材料数量呈二次增长，亟需高效替代方案。

Method: 提出代理建模框架：通过固定代理材料集C，学习每个材料A的嵌入向量z_A（由f(A,ci)映射得到），再通过融合函数p(z_A,z_B)近似f(A,B)。支持确定性和概率性实现，包含代理选择策略和缺失数据处理机制。

Result: 在模拟和实测摩擦数据集上，该方法达到高预测精度，在部分观测条件下表现稳健，显著减少了所需配对测试次数。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种紧凑、可解释且带有校准不确定性估计的摩擦系数预测方案，可有效替代大规模穷举实验。

Abstract: Accurately estimating friction coefficients between arbitrary material pairs is critical for robotics, digital fabrication, and physics-based simulation, but exhaustive pairwise testing scales quadratically with the number of materials. We introduce a proxy-based modeling framework that approximates any pairwise friction $f(A,B)$ from a small, fixed set of proxy materials $C=[c_1,\dots,c_k]$ by learning a per-material embedding $z_A = g(f(A,c1),\dots,f(A,ck))$ and a fusion function $p$ such that $f(A,B)\approx p\big(z_A,z_B\big)$. We present deterministic and probabilistic realizations of $g$ and $p$, procedures for selecting diverse proxy sets, and mechanisms for handling missing or noisy proxy measurements. The learned embeddings are compact, interpretable, and enable calibrated uncertainty estimates for downstream decision making. On simulated and measured friction datasets, our approach achieves high predictive accuracy, robust performance with partial observations, and substantial experimental savings by significantly reducing pairwise testing.

</details>


### [251] [OpenPodcar2: a robust, ROS2 vehicle for self-driving research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24242)
*Rakshit Soni,Chris Waltham,Md Umar Ibrahim,Mark Crampton,Charles Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenPodcar2是一个基于市售硬顶代步车改造的开源自动驾驶平台，集成了ROS2接口、OSH R4机电控制板、Gazebo仿真和nav2自主导航系统，总成本约2000-7000美元，速度达15km/h，可用于最后一英里自动驾驶出租车或货物运输等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶研究平台要么成本过高，要么缺乏足够的鲁棒性和实用性。前代OpenPodcar存在电子系统和ROS2接口方面的不足，需要改进以兼顾研究部署和实际应用需求。

Method: 基于硬顶代步车改造，集成OSH R4通用机电板，搭建Gazebo仿真环境，采用nav2堆栈实现SLAM和自主导航规划与控制，所有组件通过统一ROS2接口连接。

Result: 实现了可载人或等效载荷、速度达15km/h、总成本7000美元（全新部件）或2000美元（二手代步车）的鲁棒自动驾驶平台，兼具安全性、实用性和经济性。

Conclusion: OpenPodcar2在真实世界实用性、安全性、成本和鲁棒性之间取得了良好平衡，为自动驾驶研究提供了一个兼具部署潜力的开源平台。

Abstract: OpenPodcar2 is a robust, ROS2-interfaced, low-cost, open source hardware and software, autonomous vehicle platform based on an off-the-shelf, hard-canopy, mobility scooter donor vehicle. It is a modification of the previous OpenPodcar design, which extends it with robust electronics and ROS2 interfacing, to enable both research and also potential deployment use cases. The platform consists of (a) hardware components: documented as a bill of materials and build instructions; (b) integration to the general purpose OSH R4 mechatronics board and a Gazebo simulation of the vehicle, both presenting a common ROS2 interface (c) higher-level ROS2 software implementations and configurations of standard robot autonomous planning and control, including the nav2 stack which performs SLAM and enacts commands to drive the vehicle from a current to a desired pose around obstacles. OpenPodcar2 can transport a human passenger or similar load at speeds up to 15km/h, for example for use as a last-mile autonomous taxi service or to transport delivery containers similarly around a city center. It is small and safe enough to be parked in a standard research lab robust enough for some deployment cases. Total build cost was around 7,000USD from new components, or 2,000USD with a used Donor Vehicle. OpenPodcar2 thus provides a research balance between real world utility, safety, cost and robustness.

</details>


### [252] [Projected Attainable Speed Space: A Driving Efficiency Metric Connecting Instantaneous Evaluation to Travel Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24295)
*Xiaohua Zhao,Zhaowei Huang,Chen Chen,Haiyi Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Projected Attainable Speed Space (PASS)模型，通过整合运动学和空间交通信息，统一评估自动驾驶车辆在瞬时和行程层面的驾驶效率，实验验证其跨尺度一致性良好（R²=0.913）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有瞬时驾驶效率指标（如速度、相对速度、车距）无法充分捕捉交通上下文，也难以保证瞬时输出与行程层面结果之间的一致性，成为自动驾驶部署的关键障碍。

Method: 提出PASS模型，将瞬时驾驶效率分解为两个耦合要素：可用加速空间（基于理想追赶机动推导的投影可达速度）和该空间的利用率（可用加速空间的时间变化率）。通过时间聚合定义行程级效率指标，并使用驾驶模拟轨迹数据校准参数，最大化时间聚合PASS与观测旅行时间的一致性。

Result: 在10个变道事件中，时间聚合PASS与旅行时间的平均决定系数达0.913，验证了PASS在瞬时与行程层面之间的一致性评估能力。

Conclusion: PASS提供了一个统一的、基于物理的框架，同时支持自动驾驶的实时决策与长期性能分析，有效桥接了瞬时效率与行程效率之间的尺度鸿沟。

Abstract: Inefficient driving behaviors, such as overly conservative yielding, remain a key obstacle to deployment of autonomous vehicles (AVs). Instantaneous driving efficiency metrics are crucial for self-driving decision-making because they affect real-time performance evaluation and control optimization. However, commonly used indicators, including speed, relative speed, and inter-vehicle distance, are limited in capturing traffic context and in ensuring consistency between instantaneous outputs and travel-level outcomes. This study proposes the Projected Attainable Speed Space (PASS) model, a unified framework for driving efficiency assessment across instantaneous and travel-level analyses by integrating kinematic and spatial traffic information. PASS characterizes instantaneous driving efficiency with two coupled elements: potential for speed improvement (available acceleration space) and response to that potential (utilization of available acceleration space). Available acceleration space is referenced to projected attainable speed, derived from an idealized catch-up maneuver using relative speed and spacing to the leading vehicle; utilization is represented by the temporal change in available acceleration space. To ensure cross-scale consistency, time-aggregated PASS is defined as a travel-level efficiency metric. Trajectory data from a driving simulation experiment are used for parameter calibration to maximize agreement between time-aggregated PASS and observed travel times. Across 10 lane-change events, results show strong consistency, with an average coefficient of determination of 0.913, validating PASS for consistent efficiency evaluation across instantaneous and travel-level temporal scales. This study provides a unified, physically grounded framework that supports real-time decision-making and long-term performance analysis in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [253] [Pedestrians play chicken with an autonomous vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24384)
*Rakshit Soni,Charles Fox*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过真实自动驾驶车辆与人类被试的实验，首次验证了序贯博弈模型（Sequential Chicken）可解决自动驾驶汽车无条件让行行人导致的“机器人冻结问题”，并发现行人行为拟合该模型时碰撞时间价值较低，表明行人不仅避免实际碰撞，也规避个人空间侵犯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶汽车通常被编程为无条件让行行人以确保安全，但这会导致“机器人冻结问题”——行人学会在所有交互中主张优先权，使车辆停滞不前。作者希望用博弈论方法解决这一问题。

Method: 使用真实自动驾驶车辆与人类被试进行实验，在安全约束条件下评估行人与AV的交互行为，并用Sequential Chicken博弈模型拟合行人行为数据。

Result: 行人行为能够很好地拟合Sequential Chicken模型，且碰撞时间价值较低，表明行人不仅规划避免实际碰撞，也会规避个人空间侵犯（proxemic personal space penalties）。

Conclusion: 与人类驾驶员类似，AV可以通过可信的微小碰撞风险威胁来破解机器人冻结问题，而行人行为在安全约束下可用博弈模型有效描述。

Abstract: Automated vehicles (AVs) are commonly programmed to yield unconditionally to pedestrians in the interest of safety. However, this design choice can give rise to the Freezing Robot Problem in which pedestrians learn to assert priority at every interaction, causing vehicles to stall and make no progress. The game theoretic Sequential Chicken model has shown that, like human drivers, AVs can resolve this problem by trading credible threats of very small risks of collision or larger risks of less severe invasion of personal space against the value of time due to yielding delays. This paper presents the first demonstration and evaluation of this approach using a real AV with human subjects and shows that pedestrian behavior under experimentally constrained safety conditions can be well fitted by Sequential Chicken, with a low time value of collision, suggestive of their planning to avoid proxemic personal space penalties as well as actual collisions.

</details>


### [254] [FreqCache: Accelerating Embodied VLN Models with Adaptive Frequency-Guided Token Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24391)
*Zihao Zheng,Xingyue Zhou,Zhihao Mao,Songyu Sun,Lingyue Zhang,Yulong Ao,Yupu Feng,Qiongqiong Zhang,Yonghua Lin,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出FreqCache，一种基于频域引导的token缓存框架，解决VLN模型中现有视觉缓存方法因视角迁移、边缘信息缺失和时序变化导致的低效问题，实现1.59倍加速且开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的token缓存方法基于视觉域设计，在VLN模型中存在三个问题：1）视角迁移时失效；2）缺乏辅助算法时忽略关键边缘信息；3）忽视场景时序变化且缓存预算缺乏可调性。作者发现这些挑战在频域中具有不变性和可分析性，从而提出频域引导的解决方案。

Method: 提出FreqCache框架，利用频域的固有属性实现token缓存的建立、刷新和自适应调整。具体利用频域特征的稳定性来解决视觉域方法在视角迁移、边缘信息提取和时序变化方面的局限性。

Result: 实验表明FreqCache实现1.59倍速度提升，且额外开销可忽略不计，验证了频域方法在VLN token缓存中的有效性。

Conclusion: 频域引导的token缓存是解决VLN模型中视觉缓存方法局限性的有效途径，FreqCache在加速比和计算开销之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Navigation (VLN) models exhibit excellent navigation accuracy but incur high computational overhead.
