Token caching has emerged as a promising training-free strategy to reduce this cost by reusing token computation results; however, existing token caching approaches rely on visual domain methods for cacheable token selection, leading to challenges when adapted to VLN models. 1) Visual domain methods become invalid when there is viewpoint migration. 2) Visual domain methods neglect critical edge information without the aid of additional algorithms. 3) Visual domain methods overlook the temporal variation of scenarios and lack adjustability in cache budgets. In this paper, we develop detailed analyses and find that the impacts of these challenges exhibit invariance and analyzability in the frequency domain. Based on these, we propose a frequency-guided token caching framework, called FreqCache. Utilizing the inherent properties of the frequency domain, FreqCache achieves optimal token cache establishment, refreshment, and adaptive adjustment. Experiments show that FreqCache achieves 1.59x speedup with ignorable overhead, showing the effect of integrating frequency domain methods in VLN token caching.

</details>


### [255] [Characterizing Vision-Language-Action Models across XPUs: Constraints and Acceleration for On-Robot Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24447)
*Kaijun Zhou,Qiwei Chen,Da Peng,Zhiyang Li,Xijun Li,Jinyu Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在异构边缘加速器（GPU/XPU/NPU）上的部署效率，发现合适尺寸的边缘设备在成本、能耗和时间（CET）上可超越旗舰GPU，并提出DP-Cache和V-AEFusion两种优化方法，实现最高2.9倍（GPU）和6倍（边缘NPU）的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型在机器人控制中部署时受限于实时推理的成本和能耗预算，大多数评估仅依赖桌面级GPU，忽视了异构边缘加速器带来的权衡与机会，因此需要系统性的模型-硬件协同分析来探索低成本的部署方案。

Method: 1. 构建跨加速器的排行榜，在CET（成本、能量、时间）维度下评估模型-硬件配对表现；2. 通过深度性能分析，揭示VLA推理的两阶段模式（计算密集的VLM骨干 + 内存密集的Action Expert）；3. 提出DP-Cache减少扩散冗余，以及V-AEFusion实现异步流水线并行。

Result: 合适尺寸的边缘设备在成本/能效上优于旗舰GPU且满足控制速率；DP-Cache和V-AEFusion在GPU上实现最高2.9倍加速，在边缘NPU上实现最高6倍加速，成功率仅轻微下降。

Conclusion: 异构边缘加速器在VLA部署中具有显著潜力，通过模型-硬件协同分析和针对性的优化方法，可以在大幅降低成本与能耗的同时保持实时推理性能和任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are promising for generalist robot control, but on-robot deployment is bottlenecked by real-time inference under tight cost and energy budgets. Most prior evaluations rely on desktop-grade GPUs, obscuring the trade-offs and opportunities offered by heterogeneous edge accelerators (GPUs/XPUs/NPUs). We present a systematic analysis for low-cost VLA deployment via model-hardware co-characterization. First, we build a cross-accelerator leaderboard and evaluate model-hardware pairs under CET (Cost, Energy, Time), showing that right-sized edge devices can be more cost-/energy-efficient than flagship GPUs while meeting control-rate constraints. Second, using in-depth profiling, we uncover a consistent two-phase inference pattern: a compute-bound VLM backbone followed by a memory-bound Action Expert, which induces phase-dependent underutilization and hardware inefficiency. Finally, guided by these insights, we propose DP-Cache and V-AEFusion to reduce diffusion redundancy and enable asynchronous pipeline parallelism, achieving up to 2.9x speedup on GPUs and 6x on edge NPUs with only marginal success degradation. The example leaderboard website is available at: https://vla-leaderboard-01.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [256] [SPLIT: Separating Physical-Contact via Latent Arithmetic in Image-Based Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24449)
*Wadhah Zai El Amri,Nicolás Navarro-Guerrero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SPLIT方法，通过潜在空间解耦策略实现基于图像的触觉传感器仿真，支持跨传感器迁移、双向仿真，并提供了可调分辨率的有眼元网格仿真，比现有方法推理速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练触觉感知ML模型需要大量数据，但获取真实交互数据因物理复杂性和变异性而困难，因此需要有效的触觉传感器仿真方法来加速研究。

Method: 提出SPLIT方法：核心是潜在空间算术策略，显式解耦接触几何与传感器特定光学属性；结合有眼元法(FEM)软体网格仿真，支持可变分辨率，实现双向仿真（变形网格→图像，图像→网格重建）。

Result: SPLIT能适应不同DIGIT背景，无需完整重训练即可迁移至GelSight R1.5等不同传感器；推理速度快于现有替代方法；支持双向仿真。

Conclusion: SPLIT作为一种多功能触觉传感器仿真工具，可加速机器人触觉感知研究，未来有望推广至更多触觉传感器类型。

Abstract: Training machine learning models for robotic tactile sensing requires vast amounts of data, yet obtaining realistic interaction data remains a challenge due to physical complexity and variability. Simulating tactile sensors is thus a crucial step in accelerating progress. This paper presents SPLIT, a novel method for simulating image-based tactile sensors, with a primary focus on the DIGIT sensor. Central to our approach is a latent space arithmetic strategy that explicitly disentangles contact geometry from sensor-specific optical properties. Unlike methods that require recalibration for every new unit, this disentanglement allows SPLIT to adapt to diverse DIGIT backgrounds and even transfer data to distinct sensors like the GelSight R1.5 without full model retraining. Beyond this adaptability, our approach achieves faster inference speeds than existing alternatives. Furthermore, we provide a calibrated finite element method (FEM) soft-body mesh simulation with variable resolution, offering a tunable trade-off between speed and fidelity. Additionally, our algorithm supports bidirectional simulation, allowing for both the generation of realistic images from deformation meshes and the reconstruction of meshes from tactile images. This versatility makes SPLIT a valuable tool for accelerating progress in robotic tactile sensing research.

</details>


### [257] [Guiding Vector Field Generation via Score-based Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24487)
*Zirui Chen,Shiliang Guo,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Score-Induced Guiding Vector Field (SGVF)框架，利用基于分数的生成模型从数据分布中直接构建引导向量场，解决了传统GVF无法处理无序、多分支路径的局限性，并在机器人平面导航中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引导向量场假设路径为光滑有序曲线，无法处理无序、多分支或由概率模型生成的复杂路径拓扑（如分叉和伪流形），需要摆脱对人工路径分割的依赖。

Method: 利用基于分数的扩散模型从点云中学习切向量场，通过单位范数、正交性和方向一致性损失确保几何保真度和控制可行性；建立了扩散模型中的分数消失与GVF奇异性之间的对应关系。

Result: 在机器人平面导航实验中，SGVF在经典GVF失效的场景中实现了可靠的路径跟随，尤其在处理分支和尖锐曲率路径时表现出优势。

Conclusion: SGVF框架弥合了生成式建模与几何控制之间的鸿沟，为复杂拓扑路径的机器人引导提供了统一且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Guiding Vector Fields (GVFs) are a powerful tool for robotic path following. However, classical methods assume smooth, ordered curves and fail when paths are unordered, multi-branch, or generated by probabilistic models. We propose a unified framework, termed the Score-Induced Guiding Vector Field (SGVF), which leverages score-based generative modeling to construct vector fields directly from data distributions. SGVF learns tangent fields from point clouds with unit-norm, orthogonality, and directional-consistency losses, ensuring geometric fidelity and control feasibility. This approach removes the reliance on ad-hoc path segmentation and enables guidance along complex topologies such as branching and pseudo-manifolds. The study establishes a correspondence between score vanishing in diffusion models and GVF singularities and highlights representational capacity near sharp path curvatures. Experiments on robotic navigation in planar environments demonstrate that SGVF achieves reliable path following in scenarios where classical GVFs fail, underscoring its potential as a bridge between generative modeling and geometric control. Code and experiment video are available at https://github.com/czr-gif/Guiding-Vector-Field-Generation-via-Score-based-Diffusion-Model.

</details>


### [258] [Hybrid A*-Based Reverse Path-Planning of a Vehicle with Trailer System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24606)
*Xincheng Cao,Haochong Chen,Bilin Aksun-Guvenc,Levent Guvenc,Brian Link,Peter J Richmond,Dokyung Yim,Shihong Fan,John Harber*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的Hybrid A*算法，用于带拖车车辆的倒车泊车路径规划，同时考虑了防碰撞和防jackknife问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有带拖车车辆的倒车路径规划方法缺乏碰撞避免功能，且人类驾驶员难以完成该操作，因此需要一种能同时处理路径规划与碰撞避免的算法。

Method: 基于改进的Hybrid A*算法，适配车辆-拖车系统，根据系统构型动态调整可用转向输入以防止jackknife，并增加碰撞避免功能。

Result: 通过仿真案例研究验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能有效解决带拖车车辆的倒车泊车路径规划与碰撞避免问题，弥补了标准Hybrid A*算法在该领域的不足。

Abstract: Reverse parking maneuvering of a vehicle with trailer system is a difficult task to complete for human drivers due to the multi-body nature of the system and the unintuitive controls required to orientate the trailer properly. The problem is complicated with the presence of other vehicles that the trailer and its connected vehicle must avoid during the reverse parking maneuver. While path planning methods in reverse motion for vehicles with trailers exist, there is a lack of results that also offer collision avoidance as part of the algorithm. This paper hence proposes a modified Hybrid A*-based algorithm that can accommodate the vehicle-trailer system as well as collision avoidance considerations with the other vehicles and obstacles in the parking environment. One of the novelties of this proposed approach is its adaptability to the vehicle with trailer system, where limits of usable steering input that prevent the occurrence of jackknife incidents vary with respect to system configuration. The other contribution is the addition of the collision avoidance functionality which the standard Hybrid A* algorithm lacks. The method is developed and presented first, followed by simulation case studies to demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [259] [Real-time windrow detection from onboard tractor sensors for automated following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24628)
*Lorenz Gunreben,Nico Heider,Sebastian Zürner,Martin Schieck,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合立体视觉和LiDAR的多模态数据集，用于开放式自主饲草收割研究中的风窗检测。基于该数据集，作者在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上实现了实时（>20 Hz）的风窗跟随方法，并验证了低成本立体传感器在4-10米关键范围内可接近LiDAR的性能（深度一致性0.965 ± 0.021）。相关数据集和ROS 2管道已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业风窗检测系统的专有设计限制了透明性，阻碍了开放式自主饲草收割研究的发展。

Method: 1) 构建多模态数据集（立体视觉+LiDAR+GNSS），以ROS2格式发布；2) 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上实现基于质心的实时风窗跟随方法（>20 Hz）。

Result: 在4-10米关键范围内，立体视觉和LiDAR深度测量一致性高达0.965 ± 0.021，表明低成本立体传感器可接近LiDAR性能。

Conclusion: 该开源ROS 2管道为无GPS风窗检测提供了可复现的基准，支持实际自主饲草收割系统的开发。

Abstract: Proprietary design in commercial windrow-detection systems restricts transparency and limits progress in open autonomous forage-harvesting research. We present a multi-modal dataset combining stereo vision and LiDAR from tractor-mounted sensors during real baling operations. The dataset includes synchronized sensor data with GNSS trajectories, partly released as ROS2 Humble bags on Zenodo, with additional data available on request. Using this dataset, we implement a real-time (>20 Hz) centroid-based windrow-following method on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. Across the critical 4-10 m guidance range, stereo and LiDAR depth measurements show strong agreement (0.965 +/- 0.021), indicating that low-cost stereo sensors can approach LiDAR performance. Our open-source ROS 2 pipeline provides a reproducible benchmark for GPS-free windrow detection and supports development of practical autonomous forage-harvesting systems. Dataset: https://zenodo.org/records/17486318

</details>


### [260] [Computational Design and Co-Robotic Fabrication for Material Reuse in Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24648)
*Arash Adel,Daniel Ruan,Ruxin Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个将数据驱动计算设计与自适应人机协作制造装配相结合的框架，通过回收不同尺寸的旧木材（辅以新木材）实现非标准结构建造，并以Timbrelyn案例验证了该方法在促进建筑循环利用方面的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化和资源枯竭要求建筑行业从线性的"取-用-弃"模式转向循环低废实践，材料再利用可减少原料开采、降低废弃物并延长碳汇材料（如木材）的使用寿命，但异构回收材料库存带来的技术和物流挑战亟待解决。

Method: 提出一个集成框架，将数据驱动的计算设计与基于反馈的自适应人机协作制造装配相结合，能够处理不同长度和几何形状的回收木材，必要时补充标准新木材。

Result: 通过Timbrelyn案例验证了该框架的可行性，展示了木材再利用如何为建筑表达提供信息并增强其表现力。

Conclusion: 该工作推动了集成化设计-制造工作流的发展，通过自适应、反馈驱动的方法应对库存限制和回收材料不确定性，促进了新材料建筑和结构设计建造中的材料再利用。

Abstract: Climate change and resource depletion demand a shift from the dominant linear "take-make-use-dispose" paradigm of construction toward circular, low-waste practices. Material reuse offers a promising pathway by reducing raw material extraction, mitigating waste, and extending the service lifespan of carbon-sequestering materials such as timber. Realizing this potential, however, requires addressing technical and logistical challenges across both design and construction for accommodating heterogeneous, reclaimed material inventories.
  This paper presents an integrated framework that couples data-driven computational design with feedback-driven adaptive human-robot collaborative (co-robotic) fabrication and assembly to enable the realization of nonstandard structures made from reclaimed timber of varying length and geometries, supplemented with new off-the-shelf timber when necessary. The framework is validated through Timbrelyn, a built case-study installation that demonstrates how timber reuse can inform and enhance architectural expression. This work contributes to the development of integrated design-to-fabrication workflows that advance adaptive, feedback-driven methods to handle inventory constraints and reclaimed material uncertainties, facilitating material reuse in the design and construction of new buildings and structures.

</details>


### [261] [Agent-Centric Visual Reinforcement Learning under Dynamic Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24661)
*Zhengru Fang,Yu Guo,Fei Liu,Yuang Zhang,Yihang Tao,Senkang Hu,Wenbo Ding,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VDCS基准（在DeepMind控制套件上引入马尔可夫切换退化）来研究视觉强化学习在动态视觉扰动下的脆弱性，并证明重构目标会导致扰动伪影纠缠到潜在表示中；为解决此问题，提出ACO-MoE框架，通过智能体中心视角恢复专家实现感知与扰动的解耦，在VDCS上恢复95.3%的干净性能，并在DMControl泛化任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉强化学习在实际应用中容易受到动态视觉扰动（如不可预测的损坏类型变化）的影响，现有方法性能严重下降，缺乏对该问题的系统性研究和有效解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出VDCS基准，在DeepMind Control Suite上引入马尔可夫切换退化模拟非平稳扰动；2) 通过信息论分析证明重构目标会纠缠扰动伪影；3) 提出ACO-MoE框架，利用智能体中心视角的混合专家机制，通过恢复专家实现损坏恢复和任务相关前景提取，解耦感知与扰动。

Result: ACO-MoE在VDCS上恢复95.3%的干净性能，在DMControl泛化任务（随机颜色和视频背景扰动）上达到SOTA，显著优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地揭示了视觉RL在动态扰动下的失败根源，并提供了一个有效的鲁棒性提升框架，为实际部署提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Visual reinforcement learning aims to empower an agent to learn policies from visual observations, yet it remains vulnerable to dynamic visual perturbations, such as unpredictable shifts in corruption types. To systematically study this, we introduce the Visual Degraded Control Suite (VDCS), a benchmark extending DeepMind Control Suite with Markov-switching degradations to simulate non-stationary real-world perturbations. Experiments on VDCS reveal severe performance degradation in existing methods. We theoretically prove via information-theoretic analysis that this failure stems from reconstruction-based objectives inevitably entangling perturbation artifacts into latent representations. To mitigate this negative impact, we propose Agent-Centric Observations with Mixture-of-Experts (ACO-MoE) to robustify visual RL against perturbations. The proposed framework leverages unique agent-centric restoration experts, achieving restoration from corruptions and task-relevant foreground extraction, thereby decoupling perception from perturbation before being processed by the RL agent. Extensive experiments on VDCS show our ACO-MoE outperforms strong baselines, recovering 95.3% of clean performance under challenging Markov-switching corruptions. Moreover, it achieves SOTA results on DMControl Generalization with random-color and video-background perturbations, demonstrating a high level of robustness.

</details>


### [262] [Pushing Radar Odometry Beyond the Pavement: Current Capabilities and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24674)
*Shaunak Kolhe,Peng Jiang,Maggie Wigness,Philip Osteen,Timothy Overbye,Chrisitan Ellis,Srikanth Saripalli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了雷达在非结构化越野环境中的里程计估计潜力，识别了全SE(3)车辆运动、地形地面回波和稀疏不稳定特征等关键挑战，并提出了Radar-KISSICP和Radar-IMU两种基线方法，在GO数据集上验证了改进效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达具有不受天气、光照和空气中颗粒物影响的独特优势，适合非结构化环境中的定位。然而，现有工作多聚焦于城市平面环境，雷达在越野环境中的性能尚不清楚，因此本文旨在探索雷达在越野里程计中的潜力并解决相关问题。

Method: 提出两种基线方法：1) Radar-KISSICP：应用运动补偿生成3D感知雷达点云；2) Radar-IMU：利用IMU预积分来稳定扫描匹配过程。

Result: 在Great Outdoors (GO)数据集上的实验表明，所提出的基线方法在具有挑战性的越野路线上改进了轨迹估计。

Conclusion: 这些基线方法为未来越野机器人中雷达里程计的发展提供了参考基准。

Abstract: Radar offers unique advantages for localization in unstructured environments, including robustness to weather, lighting, and airborne particulates. While most prior work has studied radar odometry in urban, largely planar settings, its performance in off-road environments remains less understood. In this paper, we investigate the potential of radar for off-road odometry estimation and identify key challenges that arise from full $SE(3)$ vehicle motion, terrain-induced ground returns, and sparse or unstable features. To address these issues, we introduce two simple baselines: Radar-KISSICP, which applies motion compensation to generate 3D-aware radar pointclouds, and Radar-IMU, which leverages IMU preintegration to stabilize scan matching. Experiments on the Great Outdoors (GO) dataset demonstrate that these baselines improve trajectory estimation in challenging routes and provide a reference point for future development of radar odometry in off-road robotics.

</details>


### [263] [Learning Human-Intention Priors from Large-Scale Human Demonstrations for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24681)
*Yifan Xie,YuAn Wang,Guangyu Chen,Jinkun Liu,Yu Sun,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MoT-HRA框架，通过构建HA-2.2M大规模人机演示数据集和三分支专家架构（视觉语言、意图、精细专家），从人类视频中提取操作先验知识，提升机器人运动合理性和分布偏移下的鲁棒控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频包含丰富的操作先验，但原始观测数据混杂场景理解、人体运动和具身化动作，难以直接用于机器人学习。现有方法缺乏从异构人类视频中有效解耦并利用这些先验的机制。

Method: 1) 构建HA-2.2M数据集：通过手部中心滤波、空间重建、时间分割和语言对齐，从异构人类视频中重建出220万条动作-语言数据。2) MoT-HRA框架：视觉语言专家预测与具身无关的3D轨迹，意图专家将MANO手部运动建模为潜在人体运动先验，精细专家将意图感知表示映射为机器人动作块。共享注意力主干和只读键值传输机制实现下游控制利用先验同时限制对上游表示的干扰。

Result: 在手部运动生成、模拟操控和真实机器人任务中，MoT-HRA在运动合理性和分布偏移下的鲁棒控制方面均取得改进。

Conclusion: MoT-HRA通过层级化分解和异构人类视频先验的有效利用，提升了机器人操控的泛化能力和鲁棒性，为从人类演示学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Human videos contain rich manipulation priors, but using them for robot learning remains difficult because raw observations entangle scene understanding, human motion, and embodiment-specific action. We introduce MoT-HRA, a hierarchical vision-language-action framework that learns human-intention priors from large-scale human demonstrations. We first curate HA-2.2M, a 2.2M-episode action-language dataset reconstructed from heterogeneous human videos through hand-centric filtering, spatial reconstruction, temporal segmentation, and language alignment. On top of this dataset, MoT-HRA factorizes manipulation into three coupled experts: a vision-language expert predicts an embodiment-agnostic 3D trajectory, an intention expert models MANO-style hand motion as a latent human-motion prior, and a fine expert maps the intention-aware representation to robot action chunks. A shared-attention trunk and read-only key-value transfer allow downstream control to use human priors while limiting interference with upstream representations. Experiments on hand motion generation, simulated manipulation, and real-world robot tasks show that MoT-HRA improves motion plausibility and robust control under distribution shift.

</details>


### [264] [Passage-Aware Structural Mapping for RGB-D Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24707)
*Ali Tourani,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Saad Ejaz,David Pérez Saura,Asier Bikandi-Noya,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez,Holger Voos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向RGB-D VSLAM的通道感知结构建图方法，通过融合几何、语义和拓扑线索检测门与可通行开口，并集成到vS-Graphs框架中以增强房间连通性建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门和通道是室内机器人导航的关键结构元素，但在现代VSLAM框架中尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 将门建模为嵌入墙内的平面实体，根据与支撑墙的共面性分类为可通行/不可通行；通过两种互补策略推断通道：关键帧间相机-墙体交互的遍历证据和映射墙几何中的间断点检测。

Result: 在室内办公序列上的定性评估显示可靠的 doorway 检测能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为在BIM-informed VSLAM中利用这些结构元素奠定了基础，并已开源代码。

Abstract: Doorways and passages are critical structural elements for indoor robot navigation, yet they remain underexplored in modern Visual SLAM (VSLAM) frameworks. This paper presents a passage-aware structural mapping approach for RGB-D VSLAM that detects doors and traversable openings by jointly fusing geometric, semantic, and topological cues. Doors are modeled as planar entities embedded within walls and classified as traversable or non-traversable based on their coplanarity with the supporting wall. Passages are inferred through two complementary strategies: traversal evidence accumulated from camera-wall interactions across consecutive keyframes, and geometric opening validation based on discontinuities in the mapped wall geometry. The proposed method is integrated into vS-Graphs as a proof of concept, enriching its scene graph with passage-level abstractions and improving room connectivity modeling. Qualitative evaluations on indoor office sequences demonstrate reliable doorway detection, and the framework lays the foundation for exploiting these elements in BIM-informed VSLAM. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/snt-arg/visual_sgraphs/tree/doorway_integration.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [An Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Method for General Aviation Aircraft Based on Multi-Fidelity Digital Twin and FMEA Knowledge Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22777)
*Zhihuan Wei,Yang Hu,Xinhang Chen,Yiming Zhang,Jie Liu,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多保真数字孪生的通用航空飞机智能故障诊断框架，结合JSBSim六自由度飞行动力学仿真、FMEA驱动的故障注入、多保真残差特征提取（配对镜像残差+GRU替代预测残差）以及大语言模型增强的可解释报告生成模块。在20类故障分类任务中，配对镜像残差方案达到96.2%的Macro-F1，GRU替代方案实现4.3倍推理加速且性能仅下降0.6%。实验表明残差特征质量对诊断性能的贡献约为分类器架构的5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用航空飞机故障诊断面临真实故障数据稀缺、故障类型多样、故障特征微弱等挑战，现有方法难以同时兼顾高精度、实时性和可解释性。

Method: 构建了四模块框架：(1) 基于JSBSim的高保真飞行动力学数字孪生；(2) 基于FMEA的三层故障注入引擎模拟19种故障的物理因果传播；(3) 多保真残差计算框架——高保真路径使用配对镜像残差获取干净故障偏差信号，低保真路径使用GRU替代模型实现在线实时残差计算；(4) LLM诊断报告引擎融合FMEA知识生成可解释自然语言报告。

Result: 配对镜像残差方案在20类故障任务上达到96.2% Macro-F1；GRU替代方案实现4.3倍推理加速，性能仅下降0.6%；跨24种方案对比表明残差特征质量对诊断性能的贡献约为分类器架构的5倍。

Conclusion: 提出"残差质量优先"的设计原则，验证了多保真数字孪生在航空故障诊断中的有效性，实现了精度、实时性与可解释性的良好平衡。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis of general aviation aircraft faces challenges including scarce real fault data, diverse fault types, and weak fault signatures. This paper proposes an intelligent fault diagnosis framework based on multi-fidelity digital twin, integrating four modules: high-fidelity flight dynamics simulation, FMEA-driven fault injection, multi-fidelity residual feature extraction, and large language model (LLM)-enhanced interpretable report generation. A digital twin is constructed using the JSBSim six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) flight dynamics engine, generating 23-channel engine health monitoring data via semi-empirical sensor synthesis equations. A three-layer fault injection engine based on failure mode and effects analysis (FMEA) models the physical causal propagation of 19 engine fault types. A multi-fidelity residual computation framework comprising paired-mirror residuals and GRU surrogate prediction residuals is proposed: the high-fidelity path obtains clean fault deviation signals using nominal mirror trajectories with identical initial conditions, while the low-fidelity path achieves online real-time residual computation through a multi-step prediction GRU surrogate model. A 1D-CNN classifier performs end-to-end diagnosis of 20 fault classes. An LLM diagnostic report engine enhanced with FMEA knowledge fuses classification results, residual evidence, and domain causal knowledge to generate interpretable natural language reports. Experiments show the paired-mirror residual scheme achieves a Macro-F1 of 96.2% on the 20-class task, while the GRU surrogate scheme achieves 4.3x inference acceleration at only 0.6% performance cost. Comparison across 24 schemes reveals that residual feature quality contributes approximately 5x more to diagnostic performance than classifier architecture, establishing the "residual quality first" design principle.

</details>


### [266] [PExA: Parallel Exploration Agent for Complex Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22934)
*Tanmay Parekh,Ella Hofmann-Coyle,Shuyi Wang,Sachith Sri Ram Kothur,Srivas Prasad,Yunmo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于软件测试覆盖思想的文本转SQL方法，通过并行执行原子SQL测试用例来保证语义覆盖，在Spider 2.0 benchmark上达到70.2%执行准确率的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的文本转SQL代理在延迟与性能之间存在权衡：提升性能往往伴随着更高的延迟，反之亦然。作者希望打破这种trade-off。

Method: 将文本转SQL任务类比为软件测试覆盖问题：1) 为原始查询准备一组更简单的原子SQL测试用例；2) 并行执行这些测试用例以覆盖原始查询的语义；3) 在收集足够信息后，利用探索过的测试用例SQL来生成最终的SQL。

Result: 在Spider 2.0 benchmark上达到了70.2%的执行准确率，实现了新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 通过软件测试覆盖的思想重新定义了文本转SQL的生成过程，在性能与延迟之间取得了更好的平衡，验证了该方法在复杂SQL生成任务上的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for text-to-SQL often struggle with latency-performance trade-off, where performance improvements come at the cost of latency or vice versa. We reformulate text-to-SQL generation within the lens of software test coverage where the original query is prepared with a suite of test cases with simpler, atomic SQLs that are executed in parallel and together ensure semantic coverage of the original query. After iterating on test case coverage, the final SQL is generated only when enough information is gathered, leveraging the explored test case SQLs to ground the final generation. We validated our framework on a state-of-the-art benchmark for text-to-SQL, Spider 2.0, achieving a new state-of-the-art with 70.2% execution accuracy.

</details>


### [267] [The Power of Power Law: Asymmetry Enables Compositional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22951)
*Zixuan Wang,Xingyu Dang,Jason D. Lee,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发现了一种反直觉现象：在组合推理任务中，幂律分布的训练数据比均匀分布更有效。通过理论分析揭示，幂律采样能够诱导有益的不对称性，改善损失景观，使模型先学习高频组合再迁移到低频技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言数据服从幂律分布，大多数知识和技能出现频率极低。直觉上，对数据进行重加权或均匀化处理能帮助模型更好地学习长尾技能。作者想要验证这一直觉是否正确。

Method: 通过组合推理任务（状态追踪、多步算术）进行实验对比，并引入极简化的技能组合任务进行理论分析，证明幂律分布下训练所需数据量显著减少。

Result: 在多种组合推理任务中，幂律分布训练始终优于均匀分布训练。理论分析表明幂律采样能改善损失景观，形成从高频到低频的有效学习迁移路径。

Conclusion: 提出对"什么是有效数据分布"的替代性视角：幂律分布天然具备优势，它使模型先掌握高频组合作为"垫脚石"，从而更高效地学习罕见的长尾技能。

Abstract: Natural language data follows a power-law distribution, with most knowledge and skills appearing at very low frequency. While a common intuition suggests that reweighting or curating data towards a uniform distribution may help models better learn these long-tail skills, we find a counterintuitive result: across a wide range of compositional reasoning tasks, such as state tracking and multi-step arithmetic, training under power-law distributions consistently outperforms training under uniform distributions. To understand this advantage, we introduce a minimalist skill-composition task and show that learning under a power-law distribution provably requires significantly less training data. Our theoretical analysis reveals that power law sampling induces a beneficial asymmetry that improves the pathological loss landscape, which enables models to first acquire high-frequency skill compositions with low data complexity, which in turn serves as a stepping stone to efficiently learn rare long-tailed skills. Our results offer an alternative perspective on what constitutes an effective data distribution for training models.

</details>


### [268] [On the Existence of an Inverse Solution for Preference-Based Reductions in Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22958)
*Alessio Zaninotto,Bruno Yun,Nir Oren,Srdjan Vesic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了偏好论证框架(PAF)的逆问题：给定一个论证图、标签和语义，判断是否存在一个偏好关系能产生期望的标签。在完全语义下，对于四种最常用的偏好约简方法，该问题大多数情况下可在多项式时间内解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将偏好引入抽象论证框架，使得偏好可以控制攻击转化为击败的过程。逆问题在偏好诱导和可解释性等应用领域有重要价值。

Method: 定义PAF逆问题，输入论证图、标签和语义，输出是否存在偏好关系能产生目标标签。在完全语义下，针对四种主流偏好约简方法进行分析。

Result: 在完全语义下，大多数情况下该逆问题可以在多项式时间内求解。

Conclusion: PAF逆问题在大多数主流偏好约简方法和完全语义下具有多项式时间可解性，为偏好诱导和可解释性应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Preference-based argumentation frameworks (PAFs) extend Dung's approach to abstract argumentation (AAFs) by encoding preferences over arguments. Such preferences control the transformation of attacks into defeats, and different approaches to doing so result in different reductions from a PAF to an AAF. In this paper we consider a PAF inverse problem which takes an argumentation graph, a labelling and a semantics as an input, and outputs a ``yes" or ``no" as to whether there is a preference relation between the arguments which can yield the desired labelling. This inverse problem has applications in areas including preference elicitation and explainability. We consider this problem in the context of the four most widely-used preference based reductions under the complete semantics. We show that in most cases, the problem can be answered in polynomial time.

</details>


### [269] [Towards Causally Interpretable Wi-Fi CSI-Based Human Activity Recognition with Discrete Latent Compression and LTL Rule Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22979)
*Luca Cotti,Luca Lavazza,Marco Cominelli,Liying Han,Gaofeng Dong,Francesco Gringoli,Mani B. Srivastava,Trevor Bihl,Erik P. Blasch,Daniel O. Brigham,Kara Combs,Lance M. Kaplan,Federico Cerutti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CHARL-TRE方法，利用Gumbel-Softmax分类变分自编码器将Wi-Fi CSI压缩为离散潜在表示，再通过因果发现提取时序依赖图并转化为LTL规则，实现完全符号化的、可解释的无线人体活动识别，性能接近端到端黑盒模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在CSI-based HAR中表现优异，但其连续潜在表示不透明且难以修改；纯符号方法无法直接处理原始CSI信号。研究旨在同时满足因果可解释性、符号可控性以及对高维原始信号的直接操作需求。

Method: 1) 利用容量受控的Gumbel-Softmax分类VAE将CSI幅度窗口压缩为离散潜在表示；2) 冻结编码器作为确定性映射，生成one-hot潜在轨迹；3) 在潜在轨迹上进行因果发现，估计类别条件时序依赖图；4) 将统计支持的滞后依赖转换为LTL规则，构建纯符号确定性分类器；5) 规则在符号层面实现天线级联融合。

Result: CHARL-TRE在保持显式时序和因果结构的同时取得了有竞争力的性能，证明基于无监督离散潜在表示的确定性符号分类可作为无线HAR中端到端黑盒模型的有效替代方案。

Conclusion: 无监督离散潜在表示+因果发现+LTL规则转换的符号化分类器，能够在保持可解释性和可控性的前提下，在无线人体活动识别任务中达到接近黑盒模型的性能，为可解释无线感知提供了新范式。

Abstract: We address Human Activity Recognition (HAR) utilizing Wi-Fi Channel State Information (CSI) under the joint requirements of causal interpretability, symbolic controllability, and direct operation on high-dimensional raw signals. Deep neural models achieve strong predictive performance on CSI-based HAR (CHAR), yet rely on continuous latent representations that are opaque and difficult to modify; purely symbolic approaches, in contrast, cannot process raw CSI streams. We propose a fully automatic and strictly decoupled pipeline in which CSI magnitude windows are compressed by a categorical variational autoencoder with Gumbel-Softmax latent variables under a capacity-controlled objective, yielding a compact discrete representation. The encoder is then frozen and used as a deterministic mapping to one-hot latent trajectories. Causal discovery is performed on these trajectories to estimate class-conditional temporal dependency graphs. Statistically supported lagged dependencies are translated into Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) rules, producing a fully symbolic and deterministic classifier based solely on rule evaluation and aggregation, without any learned discriminative head. Because rules are defined over discrete latent variables, antenna-specific rule sets can in principle be combined at the symbolic level, enabling structured multi-antenna fusion without retraining the encoder. Results from CHAR Latent Temporal Rule Extraction (CHARL-TRE) indicate competitive performance while preserving explicit temporal and causal structure, showing that deterministic symbolic classification grounded in unsupervised discrete latent representations constitutes a viable alternative to end-to-end black-box models for wireless HAR.

</details>


### [270] [FormalScience: Scalable Human-in-the-Loop Autoformalisation of Science with Agentic Code Generation in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23002)
*Jordan Meadows,Lan Zhang,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FormalScience——一个领域无关的人机协同自动化形式化验证流水线，使领域专家（无需深厚形式化语言经验）能以低成本生成语法正确、语义对齐的正式证明。基于该框架构建了包含200个大学物理问题的FormalPhysics数据集（Lean4形式化表示），并系统刻画了物理自动形式化的语义漂移现象（如符号坍塌、抽象提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非正式的数学推理形式化为可验证代码是大语言模型面临的重大挑战，尤其在物理学等科学领域（狄拉克符号、向量微积分等）存在额外的形式化困难。现有的LLM和智能体方法尚未解决这些问题，需要一种低成本的自动化形式化方案。

Method: 提出FormalScience——领域无关的人机协同智能体流水线，使单个领域专家能生成语法正确且语义对齐的正式证明。具体包括：零样本提示、带错误反馈的自我改进、以及新颖的多阶段智能体方法。基于该框架构建FormalPhysics数据集（200个大学物理问题，涵盖量子力学和电磁学，使用Lean4表示）。

Result: FormalPhysics数据集实现了完美的形式化有效性，相比现有数学基准具有更高的语句复杂度。首次系统刻画了物理自动形式化中的语义漂移现象（符号坍塌、抽象提升），揭示了当完整语义保留不可实现时形式化语言实际验证的内容。

Conclusion: FormalScience为科学领域的形式化推理提供了低成本、高效率的解决方案；FormalPhysics数据集填补了物理自动形式化的空白；语义漂移的系统刻画为未来改进提供了方向；交互式UI系统可推广到物理之外的更多科学领域。

Abstract: Formalising informal mathematical reasoning into formally verifiable code is a significant challenge for large language models. In scientific fields such as physics, domain-specific machinery (\textit{e.g.} Dirac notation, vector calculus) imposes additional formalisation challenges that modern LLMs and agentic approaches have yet to tackle. To aid autoformalisation in scientific domains, we present FormalScience; a domain-agnostic human-in-the-loop agentic pipeline that enables a single domain expert (without deep formal language experience) to produce \textit{syntactically correct} and \textit{semantically aligned} formal proofs of informal reasoning for low economic cost. Applying FormalScience to physics, we construct FormalPhysics, a dataset of 200 university-level (LaTeX) physics problems and solutions (primarily quantum mechanics and electromagnetism), along with their Lean4 formal representations. Compared to existing formal math benchmarks, FormalPhysics achieves perfect formal validity and exhibits greater statement complexity. We evaluate open-source models and proprietary systems on a statement autoformalisation task on our dataset via zero-shot prompting, self-refinement with error feedback, and a novel multi-stage agentic approach, and explore autoformalisation limitations in modern LLM-based approaches. We provide the first systematic characterisation of semantic drift in physics autoformalisation in terms of concepts such as notational collapse and abstraction elevation which reveals what formal language verifies when full semantic preservation is unattainable. We release the codebase together with an interactive UI-based FormalScience system which facilitates autoformalisation and theorem proving in scientific domains beyond physics.https://github.com/jmeadows17/formal-science

</details>


### [271] [A Systematic Approach for Large Language Models Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23027)
*Basel Shbita,Anna Lisa Gentile,Bing Zhang,Sungeun An,Shailja Thakur,Shubhi Asthana,Yi Zhou,Saptha Surendran,Farhan Ahmed,Rohan Kulkarni,Yuya Jeremy Ong,Chad DeLuca,Hima Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统化的LLM调试方法，将模型视为可观察系统，通过统一的评估、可解释性和错误分析流程，实现从问题检测到模型改进的迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM因其黑箱和概率性本质，以及跨任务调试的困难，导致传统调试方法效果不佳，亟需一种结构化、模型无关的调试方法论。

Method: 提出统一评估、可解释性和错误分析的系统化方法，支持迭代式诊断模型弱点、优化提示词和模型参数、调整微调数据，且无需依赖标准基准。

Result: 该方法能在缺乏标准化基准的场景下有效工作，加速故障排查，增强可重复性、透明度和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 结构化的调试方法论对LLM系统的可靠部署至关重要，能显著提升调试效率与系统鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become central to modern AI workflows, powering applications from open-ended text generation to complex agent-based reasoning. However, debugging these models remains a persistent challenge due to their opaque and probabilistic nature and the difficulty of diagnosing errors across diverse tasks and settings. This paper introduces a systematic approach for LLM debugging that treats models as observable systems, providing structured, model-agnostic methods from issue detection to model refinement. By unifying evaluation, interpretability, and error-analysis practices, our approach enables practitioners to iteratively diagnose model weaknesses, refine prompts and model parameters, and adapt data for fine-tuning or assessment, while remaining effective in contexts where standardized benchmarks and evaluation criteria are lacking. We argue that such a structured methodology not only accelerates troubleshooting but also fosters reproducibility, transparency, and scalability in the deployment of LLM-based systems.

</details>


### [272] [A Decoupled Human-in-the-Loop System for Controlled Autonomy in Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23049)
*Edward Cheng,Jeshua Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解耦的 Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) 系统架构，将人类监督作为独立组件集成到智能体运行环境中。同时，引入了一个四维设计框架（干预条件、角色解析、交互语义、通信通道），用于形式化 HITL 集成，支持可扩展的治理和渐进式自主。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 HITL 机制通常嵌入在应用逻辑中，限制了跨多智能体环境的重用性、一致性和可扩展性。为了在智能体工作流中实现安全、可控的自主性，需要一种解耦的、可复用的 HITL 架构。

Method: 提出了一种解耦的 HITL 系统架构，将人类监督作为独立组件；引入四维设计框架：干预条件、角色解析、交互语义、通信通道；支持与智能体通信协议对齐，将 HITL 实现为协议级关注点。

Result: 该方法支持外部化 HITL 并结构化其集成，为多智能体环境中的可扩展治理和渐进式自主提供基础。

Conclusion: 解耦的 HITL 架构通过显式接口和结构化执行模型分离人类交互管理与应用工作流，能够实现选择性、上下文感知的人类参与，同时保持系统级一致性，为智能体工作流的安全治理提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed to execute tasks and make decisions within agentic workflows, introducing new requirements for safe and controlled autonomy. Prior work has established the importance of human oversight for ensuring transparency, accountability, and trustworthiness in such systems. However, existing implementations of Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) mechanisms are typically embedded within application logic, limiting reuse, consistency, and scalability across multi-agent environments.
  This paper presents a decoupled HITL system architecture that treats human oversight as an independent system component within the agent operating environment. The proposed design separates human interaction management from application workflows through explicit interfaces and a structured execution model. In addition, a design framework is introduced to formalize HITL integration along four dimensions: intervention conditions, role resolution, interaction semantics, and communication channel. This framework enables selective and context-aware human involvement while maintaining system-level consistency.
  The approach supports alignment with emerging agent communication protocols, allowing HITL to be implemented as a protocol-level concern. By externalizing HITL and structuring its integration, the system provides a foundation for scalable governance and progressive autonomy in agentic workflows.

</details>


### [273] [Don't Make the LLM Read the Graph: Make the Graph Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23057)
*Yuqi Sun,Tianqin Meng,George Liu,Yashraj Panwar,Lakshya Chaudhry,Munasib Ilham,Aman Chadha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过3000+次对照实验，研究了显式信念图在LLM多智能体协作推理中的作用。发现：集成架构决定信念图价值（作为提示上下文时仅对弱模型有效，作为动作选择的排序短列表时对强模型也至关重要）；识别出"规划者违抗"现象（模型家族特异性失败，Llama 70B达90%违抗率而Gemini近乎为零）；完整游戏证据证实智能体间约定优于所有单智能体干预；初步缩放分析表明图深度收益递减，浅层图性价比最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究显式信念图（belief graphs）能否提升LLM在多智能体协作推理中的表现，特别是在需要心智理论（Theory of Mind）的复杂协作场景中。

Method: 在Hanabi合作纸牌游戏中，跨越4个LLM家族（含Gemini、Llama等）进行3000+次受控实验。设计了两种信念图集成架构：（1）作为提示上下文输入；（2）通过排序短列表门控动作选择。同时测试了多种心智理论深度（1阶、2阶等）和玩家数量配置。

Result: （1）集成架构决定性：作为上下文时仅弱模型在2阶ToM任务获益（80% vs 10%）；门控架构下强模型也达100% vs 20%。（2）发现"规划者违抗"：Llama 70B违抗率90%，Gemini近乎零，且模型能区分事实性上下文（遵从）与建议性推荐（覆盖）。（3）智能体间约定带来+128%提升（p=0.003），超越所有单智能体干预。（4）图深度收益递减：浅层图性价比最优，深层ToM图在5人游戏中反而有害（-1.5分，p=0.029）。

Conclusion: 信念图的有效性高度依赖集成架构设计，门控机制比上下文提示更优；不同模型家族对规划推荐的违抗行为存在显著差异；多智能体协作中，智能体间约定比单方面改进更有效；在资源有限时，浅层信念图是最优选择，追求更深的心智理论层次可能适得其反。

Abstract: We investigate whether explicit belief graphs improve LLM performance in cooperative multi-agent reasoning. Through 3,000+ controlled trials across four LLM families in the cooperative card game Hanabi, we establish four findings. First, integration architecture determines whether belief graphs provide value: as prompt context, graphs are decorative for strong models and beneficial only for weak models on 2nd-order Theory of Mind (80% vs 10%, p<0.0001, OR=36.0); when graphs gate action selection through ranked shortlists, they become structurally essential even for strong models (100% vs 20% on 2nd-order ToM, p<0.001). Second, we identify "Planner Defiance," a model-family-specific failure where LLMs override correct planner recommendations at partial competence (90% override, replicated N=20); Gemini models show near-zero defiance while Llama 70B shows 90%, and models distinguish factual context (deferred to) from advisory recommendations (overridden). Third, full-game evidence confirms inter-agent conventions (+128% over baseline, p=0.003) outperform all single-agent interventions, and individual belief-graph components must be combined to produce gains. Fourth, preliminary scaling analysis (N=10/cell, exploratory) suggests graph depth has diminishing returns: shallow graphs provide the best cost-benefit ratio, while deeper ToM graphs appear harmful at larger player counts (-1.5 pts at 5-player, p=0.029).

</details>


### [274] [Analytica: Soft Propositional Reasoning for Robust and Scalable LLM-Driven Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23072)
*Junyan Cheng,Kyle Richardson,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Analytica，一种基于软命题推理（SPR）的新型智能体架构，通过将复杂分析分解为子命题树、结合工具增强的接地智能体（含Jupyter Notebook智能体）进行事实验证，并利用稳健线性模型递归合成结果，在多个预测任务上平均提升15.84%准确率，同时降低方差和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在复杂分析中推理不稳定、缺乏可验证的组合结构，难以保证结果的可靠性和可解释性。

Method: 采用软命题推理（SPR）框架，将分析问题分解为子命题树，使用工具增强的LLM接地智能体（包括Jupyter Notebook智能体）进行事实验证以降低偏差，再通过稳健线性模型递归合成叶子节点以降低方差，支持交互式"what-if"分析。

Result: 在经济、金融和政治预测任务上，平均提升15.84%准确率，达71.06%准确率且方差最低6.02%；Jupyter Notebook接地智能体以降低90.35%成本和52.85%时间实现70.11%准确率；具有近线性时间复杂度和良好的噪声鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Analytica通过结构化分解与稳健合成有效提升了LLM智能体在复杂分析任务中的准确性、稳定性和成本效率，并展现出对开源模型和科学领域的良好适应性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly tasked with complex real-world analysis (e.g., in financial forecasting, scientific discovery), yet their reasoning suffers from stochastic instability and lacks a verifiable, compositional structure. To address this, we introduce Analytica, a novel agent architecture built on the principle of Soft Propositional Reasoning (SPR). SPR reframes complex analysis as a structured process of estimating the soft truth values of different outcome propositions, allowing us to formally model and minimize the estimation error in terms of its bias and variance. Analytica operationalizes this through a parallel, divide-and-conquer framework that systematically reduces both sources of error. To reduce bias, problems are first decomposed into a tree of subpropositions, and tool-equipped LLM grounder agents are employed, including a novel Jupyter Notebook agent for data-driven analysis, that help to validate and score facts. To reduce variance, Analytica recursively synthesizes these grounded leaves using robust linear models that average out stochastic noise with superior efficiency, scalability, and enable interactive "what-if" scenario analysis. Our theoretical and empirical results on economic, financial, and political forecasting tasks show that Analytica improves 15.84% accuracy on average over diverse base models, achieving 71.06% accuracy with the lowest variance of 6.02% when working with a Deep Research grounder. Our Jupyter Notebook grounder shows strong cost-effectiveness that achieves a close 70.11% accuracy with 90.35% less cost and 52.85% less time. Analytica also exhibits highly noise-resilient and stable performance growth as the analysis depth increases, with a near-linear time complexity, as well as good adaptivity to open-weight LLMs and scientific domains.

</details>


### [275] [Towards Automated Ontology Generation from Unstructured Text: A Multi-Agent LLM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23090)
*Abid Talukder,Maruf Ahmed Mridul,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体架构（领域专家、项目经理、编码员、质量审查员），用于从非结构化自然语言自动生成正式本体。在保险合同领域实验中，相比单智能体LLM基线，该方法通过前置规划显著提升了结构质量和可查询性，为可扩展的自动化本体工程提供了有前景的审计路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非结构化自然语言自动生成正式本体是知识工程的长期挑战。尽管LLM展现出潜力，但哪些架构设计选择驱动生成质量、当前方法为何失败尚不明确。

Method: 采用受控实验：1) 建立单智能体LLM基线，识别失败模式；2) 提出多智能体架构，将本体构建分解为四个基于工件的角色（领域专家、项目经理、编码员、质量审查员）；3) 通过异构LLM评审面板评估架构质量，结合基于竞争性问题的SPARQL评估和RAG评估功能可用性。

Result: 多智能体方法在结构质量上显著优于基线，查询能力也有适度提升。性能提升主要归功于前置规划（front-loaded planning）。

Conclusion: 以规划优先、工件驱动的方式生成本体是一种有前景且更可审计的路径，有望实现可扩展的自动化本体工程。

Abstract: Automatically generating formal ontologies from unstructured natural language remains a central challenge in knowledge engineering. While large language models (LLMs) show promise, it remains unclear which architectural design choices drive generation quality and why current approaches fail. We present a controlled experimental study using domain-specific insurance contracts to investigate these questions. We first establish a single-agent LLM baseline, identifying key failure modes such as poor Ontology Design Pattern compliance, structural redundancy, and ineffective iterative repair. We then introduce a multi-agent architecture that decomposes ontology construction into four artifact-driven roles: Domain Expert, Manager, Coder, and Quality Assurer. We evaluate performance across architectural quality (via a panel of heterogeneous LLM judges) and functional usability (via competency question driven SPARQL evaluation with complementary retrieval augmented generation based assessment). Results show that the multi-agent approach significantly improves structural quality and modestly enhances queryability, with gains driven primarily by front-loaded planning. These findings highlight planning-first, artifact-driven generation as a promising and more auditable path toward scalable automated ontology engineering.

</details>


### [276] [PhySE: A Psychological Framework for Real-Time AR-LLM Social Engineering Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23148)
*Tianlong Yu,Yang Yang,Ziyi Zhou,Jiaying Xu,Siwei Li,Tong Guan,Kailong Wang,Ting Bi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PhySE框架，通过VLM预训练消除冷启动延迟，并引入自适应心理Agent动态调整社交工程策略，在60人用户研究中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR-LLM社交工程攻击（如SEAR）威胁现实社交安全，但现有方法存在两大瓶颈：冷启动个性化延迟（RAG方法在初始轮次耗时）和静态攻击策略（缺乏心理学理论基础）。因此需要一种实时、动态的社交工程框架。

Method: 提出PhySE框架，包含两个核心创新：1）VLM-Based SocialContext Training：利用社交情境数据预训练视觉语言模型，实现即时画像生成，消除RAG的冷启动延迟；2）Adaptive Psychological Agent：引入心理学LLM，根据目标回应动态部署不同类别的心理策略，替代静态手工脚本。

Result: 通过IRB批准的60人用户研究，收集了360个跨多种社交场景的带标注对话数据集，验证了PhySE的有效性。

Conclusion: PhySE通过VLM预训练和自适应心理Agent，有效解决了AR-LLM社交工程攻击中的冷启动延迟和策略静态问题，为实时动态社交工程提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: The emerging threat of AR-LLM-based Social Engineering (AR-LLM-SE) attacks (e.g. SEAR) poses a significant risk to real-world social interactions. In such an attack, a malicious actor uses Augmented Reality (AR) glasses to capture a target visual and vocal data. A Large Language Model (LLM) then analyzes this data to identify the individual and generate a detailed social profile. Subsequently, LLM-powered agents employ social engineering strategies, providing real-time conversation suggestions, to gain the target trust and ultimately execute phishing or other malicious acts. Despite its potential, the practical application of AR-LLM-SE faces two major bottlenecks, (1) Cold-start personalization, Current Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) methods introduce critical delays in the earliest turns, slowing initial profile formation and disrupting real-time interaction, (2) Static Attack Strategies, Existing approaches rely on fixed-stage, handcrafted social engineering tactics that lack foundation in established psychological theory. To address these limitations, we propose PhySE, a novel framework with two core innovations, (1) VLM-Based SocialContext Training, To eliminate profiling delays, we efficiently pre-train a Visual Language Model (VLM) with social-context data, enabling rapid, on-the-fly profile generation, (2) Adaptive Psychological Agent, We introduce a psychological LLM that dynamically deploys distinct classes of psychological strategies based on target response, moving beyond static, handcrafted scripts. We evaluated PhySE through an IRB-approved user study with 60 participants, collecting a novel dataset of 360 annotated conversations across diverse social scenarios.

</details>


### [277] [Judging the Judges: A Systematic Evaluation of Bias Mitigation Strategies in LLM-as-a-Judge Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23178)
*Sadman Kabir Soumik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了9种去偏策略在5种LLM裁判模型上的表现，发现风格偏差（0.76-0.92）远超位置偏差（≤0.04），所有模型偏好简洁性，而去偏策略效果依赖模型，其中预算组合策略使Claude Sonnet 4提升11.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge已成为评估语言模型输出的主流范式，但LLM裁判存在系统性偏差，影响评估可靠性。目前对多种偏差类型和去偏策略缺乏全面的实证比较。

Method: 在5种裁判模型（来自Google、Anthropic、OpenAI、Meta）、3个基准（MT-Bench n=400、LLMBar n=200、自定义n=225）和4种偏差类型上，比较9种去偏策略。设计截断控制实验区分质量与长度偏好，并采用预算组合策略进行去偏。

Result: (1)风格偏差是主导偏差（0.76-0.92），远超位置偏差（≤0.04）；(2)所有模型偏好简洁性，但能正确区分质量与长度（准确率0.92-1.00）；(3)预算组合策略使Claude Sonnet 4提升11.2个百分点（p<0.0001），仅2/20非基线配置出现一致性下降。

Conclusion: 去偏有益但依赖模型，风格偏差应获得更多研究关注；模型对质量的敏感度高于简单的长度偏差。研究提供了评估框架和实验数据以推动该领域发展。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge has become the dominant paradigm for evaluating language model outputs, yet LLM judges exhibit systematic biases that compromise evaluation reliability. We present a comprehensive empirical study comparing nine debiasing strategies across five judge models from four provider families (Google, Anthropic, OpenAI, Meta), three benchmarks (MT-Bench n=400, LLMBar n=200, custom n=225), and four bias types. Our key findings: (1) Style bias is the dominant bias (0.76-0.92 across all models), far exceeding position bias (<= 0.04), yet has received minimal research attention. (2) All models show a conciseness preference on expansion pairs, but truncation controls confirm they correctly distinguish quality from length (0.92-1.00 accuracy), suggesting quality-sensitive evaluation rather than a simple length bias. (3) Debiasing is beneficial but model-dependent: the combined budget strategy significantly improves Claude Sonnet 4 by +11.2 pp (p < 0.0001), with directionally positive trends for other models. Only 2 of 20 non-baseline configurations show decreased agreement. We release our evaluation framework, controlled dataset, and all experimental artifacts at https://github.com/sksoumik/llm-as-judge.

</details>


### [278] [From Coarse to Fine: Self-Adaptive Hierarchical Planning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23194)
*Haoran Tan,Zeyu Zhang,Chen Ma,Tianze Liu,Quanyu Dai,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AdaPlan-H，一种受认知科学启发的自适应分层规划机制，通过渐进式细化从粗粒度宏计划开始，根据任务复杂度动态调整粒度级别，在复杂任务中提升成功率并减少过度规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体规划方法在固定的粒度级别上运作，简单任务提供过多细节（过度规划），复杂任务细节不足，无法在简洁性与复杂度之间取得最佳平衡。

Method: 受认知科学中渐进式细化原理启发，AdaPlan-H从粗粒度宏计划开始，根据任务复杂度逐步细化计划，生成自适应分层计划，并通过模仿学习和能力增强进行优化。

Result: 该方法显著提升了任务执行成功率，同时减少了规划层面的过度规划问题。

Conclusion: AdaPlan-H为多步骤复杂决策任务提供了一种灵活高效的解决方案，代码和数据将开源。

Abstract: Large language model-based agents have recently emerged as powerful approaches for solving dynamic and multi-step tasks. Most existing agents employ planning mechanisms to guide long-term actions in dynamic environments. However, current planning approaches face a fundamental limitation that they operate at a fixed granularity level. Specifically, they either provide excessive detail for simple tasks or insufficient detail for complex ones, failing to achieve an optimal balance between simplicity and complexity. Drawing inspiration from the principle of \textit{progressive refinement} in cognitive science, we propose \textbf{AdaPlan-H}, a self-adaptive hierarchical planning mechanism that mimics human planning strategies. Our method initiates with a coarse-grained macro plan and progressively refines it based on task complexity. It generates self-adaptive hierarchical plans tailored to the varying difficulty levels of different tasks, which can be optimized by imitation learning and capability enhancement. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly improves task execution success rates while mitigating overplanning at the planning level, providing a flexible and efficient solution for multi-step complex decision-making tasks. To contribute to the community, our code and data will be made publicly available at https://github.com/import-myself/AHP.

</details>


### [279] [StoryTR: Narrative-Centric Video Temporal Retrieval with Theory of Mind Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23198)
*Xuanyue Zhong,Yuqiang Xie,Guanqun Bi,Jiangping Yang,Guibin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StoryTR，首个需要心智理论(ToM)推理的视频时刻检索基准，包含8100个叙事短视频样本。针对现有模型只能理解"发生什么"而无法推理"为什么重要"的问题，提出Agentic数据流水线生成带有三层ToM链的训练数据。7B参数的Shorts-Moment模型在ToM引导数据上训练后，相对IoU提升15.1%，证明叙事推理能力比参数规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时刻检索在动作类任务上表现良好，但难以处理叙事内容。模型能够识别"发生了什么"，却无法理解"为什么重要"。这种语义鸿沟源于缺乏Theory of Mind（心智理论）——从表面观察推断隐含意图、心理状态和叙事因果关系的认知能力。

Method: 1. 构建StoryTR基准：8100个叙事短视频样本，要求ToM推理。2. 提出Agentic Data Pipeline，生成带有三层ToM链的训练数据：意图解码(Intent Decoding)、叙事推理(Narrative Reasoning)、边界定位(Boundary Localization)。3. 训练Shorts-Moment模型（7B参数）。

Result: Gemini-3.0-Pro在StoryTR上仅达到0.53 Avg IoU，揭示严重推理差距。7B参数的Shorts-Moment模型在ToM引导数据上训练后，相对IoU提升15.1%。

Conclusion: 叙事推理能力比参数规模更重要。通过ToM引导的数据训练，较小模型也能显著超越大型模型，为视频叙事理解领域提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Current video moment retrieval excels at action-centric tasks but struggles with narrative content. Models can see \textit{what is happening} but fail to reason \textit{why it matters}. This semantic gap stems from the lack of \textbf{Theory of Mind (ToM)}: the cognitive ability to infer implicit intentions, mental states, and narrative causality from surface-level observations. We introduce \textbf{StoryTR}, the first video moment retrieval benchmark requiring ToM reasoning, comprising 8.1k samples from narrative short-form videos (shorts/reels). These videos present an ideal testbed. Their high information density encodes meaning through subtle multimodal cues. For instance, a glance paired with a sigh carries entirely different semantics than the glance alone. Yet multimodal perception alone is insufficient; ToM is required to decode that a character ``smiling'' may actually be ``concealing hostility.'' To teach models this reasoning capability, we propose an \textbf{Agentic Data Pipeline} that generates training data with explicit three-tier ToM chains (intent decoding, narrative reasoning, boundary localization). Experiments reveal the severity of the reasoning gap: Gemini-3.0-Pro achieves only 0.53 Avg IoU on StoryTR. However, our 7B \textbf{Shorts-Moment} model, trained on ToM-guided data, improves +15.1\% relative IoU over baselines, demonstrating that \textit{narrative reasoning capability matters more than parameter scale}.

</details>


### [280] [Discovering Agentic Safety Specifications from 1-Bit Danger Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23210)
*Víctor Gallego*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EPO-Safe框架证明，LLM智能体仅通过稀疏的二值危险信号就能自主发现隐藏的安全目标，在1-2轮迭代内生成可读的安全规范。研究表明，仅依赖奖励的反思会加剧奖励破解行为，而专用的安全通道是发现隐藏约束的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM反思方法依赖丰富的文本反馈（如编译器错误等），但实际安全场景中智能体可能只能获得极度稀疏的信号（如一个比特表示是否危险）。作者探究LLM能否仅通过这种贫瘠信号发现隐藏的安全目标，并揭示纯奖励驱动的反思反而会损害安全性。

Method: 提出EPO-Safe框架：LLM迭代生成行动方案，接收稀疏的二值危险警告（每时间步仅一个比特），通过反思演化出自然语言行为规范。在5个AI Safety Gridworlds和5个文本场景中进行评估，其中可见奖励R与隐藏安全目标R*可能不一致。

Result: EPO-Safe在1-2轮（5-15个回合）内发现安全行为，生成可读规范并包含正确的危险假设（如"从北进入X格子是危险的"）。即使50%的非危险步骤产生虚假警告，平均安全性能仅下降15%。相反，纯奖励驱动的反思会加速奖励破解行为。

Conclusion: EPO-Safe证明了从极度稀疏信号中学习安全规范的可行性，生成的规范可作为可审计的行为规则集。反思必须与专用的安全通道配对，而非仅依赖奖励信号。该方法在噪声环境下具有鲁棒性，为自主安全对齐提供了新路径。

Abstract: Can large language model agents discover hidden safety objectives through experience alone? We introduce EPO-Safe (Experiential Prompt Optimization for Safe Agents), a framework where an LLM iteratively generates action plans, receives sparse binary danger warnings, and evolves a natural language behavioral specification through reflection. Unlike standard LLM reflection methods that rely on rich textual feedback (e.g., compiler errors or detailed environment responses), EPO-Safe demonstrates that LLMs can perform safety reasoning from a strictly impoverished signal in structured, low-dimensional environments: the agent never observes the hidden performance function $R^*$, only a single bit per timestep indicating that an action was unsafe. We evaluate on five AI Safety Gridworlds (Leike et al., 2017) and five text-based scenario analogs where visible reward $R$ may diverge from $R^*$. EPO-Safe discovers safe behavior within 1-2 rounds (5-15 episodes), producing human-readable specifications with correct explanatory hypotheses about hazards (e.g., "X cells are directionally hazardous: entering from the north is dangerous"). Critically, we show that standard reward-driven reflection actively degrades safety: agents reflecting on reward alone use the loop to justify and accelerate reward hacking, proving that reflection must be paired with a dedicated safety channel to discover hidden constraints. We further evaluate robustness to noisy oracles: even when 50% of non-dangerous steps produce spurious warnings, mean safety performance degrades by only 15% on average, though sensitivity is environment-dependent, as cross-episode reflection naturally filters inconsistent signals. Each evolved specification functions as an auditable set of grounded behavioral rules discovered autonomously through interaction, rather than authored by humans as in Constitutional AI (Bai et al., 2022).

</details>


### [281] [AdaMamba: Adaptive Frequency-Gated Mamba for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23239)
*Xudong Jiang,Mingshan Loo,Hanchen Yang,Wengen Li,Mingrui Zhang,Yichao Zhang,Jihong Guan,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdaMamba是一个将自适应频域分析集成到Mamba状态空间模型中的长时序预测框架，通过在状态更新中引入频率门控机制，有效捕获跨域异质性，在7个公开基准和2个领域数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有频域LTSF方法隐式假设时间域和频率域具有同质性，无法适应现实时序中变量在时域同步但在频域差异显著的跨域异质性。

Method: 提出AdaMamba框架：(1) 交互式补丁编码模块捕获变量间交互；(2) 自适应频率门控状态空间模块生成输入依赖的频率基，将传统时序遗忘门扩展为统一的时频遗忘门，动态校准状态转换。

Result: 在7个公开LTSF基准和2个领域特定数据集上，AdaMamba在预测准确度上持续优于SOTA方法，同时保持具有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 将自适应频域分析内生于状态空间模型的更新过程，是提升长时序预测性能的有效路径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate long-term time series forecasting (LTSF) requires the capture of complex long-range dependencies and dynamic periodic patterns. Recent advances in frequency-domain analysis offer a global perspective for uncovering temporal characteristics. However, real-world time series often exhibit pronounced cross-domain heterogeneity where variables that appear synchronized in the time domain can differ substantially in the frequency domain. Existing frequency-based LTSF methods often rely on implicit assumptions of cross-domain homogeneity, which limits their ability to adapt to such intricate variability. To effectively integrate frequency-domain analysis with temporal dependency learning, we propose AdaMamba, a novel framework that endogenizes adaptive and context-aware frequency analysis within the Mamba state-space update process. Specifically, AdaMamba introduces an interactive patch encoding module to capture inter-variable interaction dynamics. Then, we develop an adaptive frequency-gated state-space module that generates input-dependent frequency bases, and generalizes the conventional temporal forgetting gate into a unified time-frequency forgetting gate. This allows dynamic calibration of state transitions based on learned frequency-domain importance, while preserving Mamba's capability in modeling long-range dependencies. Extensive experiments on seven public LTSF benchmarks and two domain-specific datasets demonstrate that AdaMamba consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in forecasting accu racy while maintaining competitive computational efficiency. The code of AdaMamba is available at https://github.com/XDjiang25/AdaMamba.

</details>


### [282] [CAP-CoT: Cycle Adversarial Prompt for Improving Chain of Thoughts in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23270)
*Shuxu Chen,Yitian Zhou,Jiaquan Zhang,Haoyu Bian,Aming Wu,Sungyoung Lee,Chaoning Zhang,Hyundong Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAP-CoT通过循环对抗提示优化框架，在2-3个周期内显著提升CoT推理的准确性和稳定性，减少跨运行变异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT推理在多步长问题上跨运行不稳定，且多数工作关注单次前向推理改进，缺乏迭代性和对比性纠正机制。

Method: 提出CAP-CoT框架，包含前向求解器生成候选推理链、对抗挑战者构造故意错误的推理链、反馈代理对比两条链并生成结构化反馈，通过双向更新求解器和挑战者提示实现循环优化。

Result: 在6个基准测试和4个LLM骨干网络上，2-3个对抗提示优化周期内，显著降低跨运行变异性，同时提升推理准确性和对提示扰动的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CAP-CoT有效解决了CoT推理的不稳定性问题，其对抗组件是任务语义导向的，不同于安全导向的对抗提示，能暴露推理链中的逻辑漏洞。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting has emerged as a simple and effective way to elicit step-by-step solutions from large language models (LLMs). However, CoT reasoning can be unstable across runs on long, multi-step problems, leading to inconsistent answers for unchanged task. Most prior work focuses on improving the forward reasoning chain within a single pass, with less attention to iterative and contrastive correction. To address this gap, we propose CAP-CoT, a Cycle Adversarial Prompt optimization framework designed to improve both CoT reasoning accuracy and stability of a single deployed solver. In each cycle, a forward solver generates candidate reasoning chains, an adversarial challenger constructs plausible but deliberately flawed chains using targeted error strategies, and a feedback agent contrasts the two chains and produces step-aligned structured feedback. This feedback closes the optimization loop in two directions, including updating the solver prompt based on errors exposed by the challenger, and updating the challenger prompt to generate increasingly targeted errors in subsequent cycles. Unlike safety-oriented adversarial prompting such as jailbreak or prompt-injection attacks, our adversarial component is task-semantic and aims to expose logical vulnerabilities in reasoning chains. Experiments across six benchmarks and four LLM backbones demonstrate that within two to three adversarial prompt optimization cycles, CAP-CoT consistently reduces variability across runs while improving reasoning accuracy and robustness to prompt perturbations.

</details>


### [283] [Active Inference: A method for Phenotyping Agency in AI systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23278)
*Philip Wilson,Axel Constant,Mahault Albarracin,Nicolás Hinrichs,Jasmine Moore,Daniel Polani,Karl Friston*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）与变分自由能原理的最小化智能体概念框架，将意图性、理性与可解释性作为智能体的三个核心判据，并通过T-maze实验以"赋权"（empowerment）作为操作化指标区分不同智能等级，最终探讨AI治理策略需从外部约束转向内部先验偏好调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体AI的发展已超越现有概念工具对计算系统中"智能体性"的表征能力，主流定义仅依赖自主性和目标导向性，亟需一种可进行原则性检验的最小化智能体概念。

Method: 提出三个判据（基于信念与欲望的意图性、基于世界模型的规范性理性、可因果追溯至内部状态的可解释性）并将其形式化为变分框架下的POMDP，以后验信念、先验偏好和期望自由能最小化构成智能体行动链；通过T-maze范式操纵生成模型结构，以"赋权"（行动与预期观察间的信道容量）作为操作化指标区分不同智能表型。

Result: 通过T-maze实验验证了赋权作为操作化指标可以有效区分零、中、高三种智能表型。

Conclusion: 随着智能体通过认知觅食解决不确定性，有效的治理控制必须从外部约束系统性地转向内部先验偏好的调节，为从计算表型到AI治理策略提供了原则性的变分桥梁。

Abstract: The proliferation of agentic artificial intelligence has outpaced the conceptual tools needed to characterize agency in computational systems. Prevailing definitions mainly rely on autonomy and goal-directedness. Here, we argue for a minimal notion open to principled inspection given three criteria: intentionality as action grounded in beliefs and desires, rationality as normatively coherent action entailed by a world model, and explainability as action causally traceable to internal states; we subsequently instantiate these as a partially observable Markov decision process under a variational framework wherein posterior beliefs, prior preferences, and the minimization of expected free energy jointly constitute an agentic action chain. Using a canonical T-maze paradigm, we evidence how empowerment, formulated as the channel capacity between actions and anticipated observations, serves as an operational metric that distinguishes zero-, intermediate-, and high-agency phenotypes through structural manipulations of the generative model. We conclude by arguing that as agents engage in epistemic foraging to resolve ambiguity, the governance controls that remain effective must shift systematically from external constraints to the internal modulation of prior preferences, offering a principled, variational bridge from computational phenotyping to AI governance strategy

</details>


### [284] [AI Identity: Standards, Gaps, and Research Directions for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23280)
*Takumi Otsuka,Kentaroh Toyoda,Alex Leung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了AI Agent在无人类持续监督下运行时面临的“AI身份”问题，指出当前基础设施无法识别、验证和追责无实体、无持续记忆、无法定地位的AI实体。研究通过行业趋势、新兴标准和技术文献的结构化调查，进行了完整的身份生命周期差距分析，发现人类身份框架无法直接适用于AI，并识别出五个关键结构性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent正在跨组织边界执行真实交易和工作流，但现有基础设施无法解决无实体、无持续记忆、无法定地位的AI实体的身份识别、验证和问责问题。

Method: 通过结构化调查行业趋势、新兴标准和技术文献，对AI Agent身份生命周期进行差距分析。

Result: (1) 从基质、持久性、可验证性和法律地位四个维度对比人类与AI身份，发现根本性不对称； (2) 评估现有技术和监管文件，发现均未充分解决非确定性、跨边界实体的治理问题； (3) 识别出五个关键差距：语义意图验证、递归委派问责、Agent身份完整性、治理透明度与执行、运营可持续性。

Conclusion: 这些差距是结构性的，仅靠工程手段无法弥合，AI身份的基础研究是解决这一问题的核心方向。

Abstract: AI agents are now running real transactions, workflows, and sub-agent chains across organizational boundaries without continuous human supervision. This creates a problem no current infrastructure is equipped to solve: how do you identify, verify, and hold accountable an entity with no body, no persistent memory, and no legal standing? We define AI Identity as the continuous relationship between what an AI agent is declared to be and what it is observed to do, bounded by the confidence that those two things correspond at any given moment. Through a structured survey of industry trends, emerging standards, and technical literature, we conduct a gap analysis across the full agent identity lifecycle and make three contributions: (1) a structural comparison of human and AI identity across four dimensions (substrate, persistence, verifiability, and legal standing) showing that the asymmetry is fundamental and that extending human frameworks to agents without structural modification produces systematic failures; (2) an evaluation of current technical and regulatory documents against the identity requirements of autonomous agents, finding that none adequately address the challenge of governing nondeterministic, boundary-crossing entities; and (3) identification of five critical gaps (semantic intent verification, recursive delegation accountability, agent identity integrity, governance opacity and enforcement, and operational sustainability) that no current technology or regulatory instrument resolves. These gaps are structural; more engineering effort alone will not close them. Foundational research on AI identity is the central conclusion of this report.

</details>


### [285] [LEGO: An LLM Skill-Based Front-End Design Generation Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23355)
*Jincheng Lou,Ruohan Xu,Jiecheng Ma,Runzhe Tao,Xinyu Qu,Yibo Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEGO 是一个基于技能的模块化前端设计生成平台，将数字前端流程分解为六个独立步骤，构建了包含42个可执行电路技能的库。在VerilogEval v2难题上，单个技能将Pass@1从0.000提升至0.805，跨项目技能组合也达到0.805，优于多个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的EDA代理往往是孤立的、任务特定的系统，导致重复工程劳动，且成功的设计与调试策略难以复用。

Method: 将数字前端流程分解为六步有限状态机，构建标准化可组合的电路技能库；提出Circuit Skill Builder实现技能自动化提取（线性可扩展），以及Agent Skill RAG实现亚毫秒级检索（无需嵌入模型）。

Result: 在gpt-5.2-codex无法解决的41个VerilogEval v2难题上，单个技能Pass@1从0.000提升至0.805（80.5%提升）；跨项目技能组合达0.805，优于hierarchy-verilog 14.6%和VerilogCoder 2.5%，与MAGE持平。

Conclusion: 模块化技能组合能够支持有效且灵活的RTL设计自动化，LEGO平台及所有电路技能均已开源。

Abstract: Existing LLM-based EDA agents are often isolated task-specific systems. This leads to repeated engineering effort and limited reuse of successful design and debugging strategies. We present LEGO, a unified skill-based platform for front-end design generation. It decomposes the digital front-end flow into six independent steps and represents every agent capability as a standardized composable circuit skill within a plug-and-play architecture. To build this skill library, we survey more than 100 papers, select 11 representative open-source projects, and extract 42 executable circuit skills within a six-step finite state machine formulation. Circuit Skill Builder automates skill extraction with linear scalability. Agent Skill RAG achieves submillisecond retrieval without relying on embedding models. Empirical evaluation on a hard subset of 41 VerilogEval v2 problems that gpt-5.2-codex fails to solve under extra-high reasoning effort shows that individual circuit skills constructed within LEGO raise Pass@1 from 0.000 to 0.805. This is an 80.5% gain over the baseline. Cross-project skill compositions also reach 0.805 Pass@1. They outperform hierarchy-verilog by 14.6% and VerilogCoder by 2.5%. They also match MAGE. These results show that modular skill composition supports both effective and flexible RTL design automation. The LEGO platform and all circuit skills are publicly available at GitHub: https://github.com/loujc/LEGO-An-LLM-Skill-Based-Front-End-Design-Generation-Platform

</details>


### [286] [GSAR: Typed Grounding for Hallucination Detection and Recovery in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23366)
*Federico A. Kamelhar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GSAR是一个面向多智能体LLM系统的可解释性框架，它通过四类证据类型划分、加权评分、不对称矛盾惩罚、三层决策函数和有限迭代外循环，实现了细粒度的接地性评估与重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接地性评估方法（二元分类器、LLM评判标量、自我纠错循环）将支持证据视为可互换的，输出单一信号，无法为下游行动提供原理性控制；且缺乏对非冗余替代视角（互补证据）的一等公民地位。

Method: (i) 将声明划分为四类（grounded、ungrounded、contradicted、complementary）；(ii) 基于证据类型赋予权重（反映认识论强度）；(iii) 计算非对称矛盾惩罚加权接地性得分；(iv) 耦合适应得分的三层决策函数（proceed/regenerate/replan），驱动有界迭代外循环，在显式计算预算下运行。

Result: 在FEVER数据集和黄金维基证据上，使用四种独立LLM评判器（gpt-5.4、claude-sonnet-4-6、claude-opus-4-7、gemini-2.5-pro）验证了五个设计主张：所有消融实验在每一评判器上方向一致，bootstrap 95%置信区间排除零，无互补消融在Opus 4.7下CI为[-96,-68]，n=1000时三个独立评判器收敛到ΔS=+0.058。与Vectara HHEM-2.1-Open进行了对比。

Conclusion: GSAR是首个将证据类型化评分与分层恢复策略结合、并在显式计算预算下运行的公开接地性评估框架，具有可解释性和可控性优势。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent LLM systems are increasingly deployed to investigate operational incidents and produce structured diagnostic reports. Their trustworthiness hinges on whether each claim is grounded in observed evidence rather than model-internal inference. Existing groundedness evaluators (binary classifiers, LLM-as-judge scalars, self-correction loops) treat supporting evidence as interchangeable and emit a single signal that offers no principled control over downstream action.
  We present GSAR, a grounding-evaluation and replanning framework that (i) partitions claims into a four-way typology (grounded, ungrounded, contradicted, complementary), giving first-class standing to non-redundant alternative perspectives; (ii) assigns evidence-type-specific weights reflecting epistemic strength; (iii) computes an asymmetric contradiction-penalised weighted groundedness score; and (iv) couples that score to a three-tier decision function (proceed, regenerate, replan) driving a bounded-iteration outer loop under an explicit compute budget.
  We formalise the algorithm, prove six structural properties, and evaluate five design claims on FEVER with gold Wikipedia evidence under four independently-trained LLM judges (gpt-5.4, claude-sonnet-4-6, claude-opus-4-7, gemini-2.5-pro). Every ablation reproduces in the same direction on every judge: bootstrap 95% CIs on the rho=0 effect exclude 0 on all four; the no-complementary ablation under Opus 4.7 has CI [-96,-68] of 200; at n=1000 three independent judges converge to DeltaS(rho=0)=+0.058. A head-to-head against Vectara HHEM-2.1-Open is included. To our knowledge, GSAR is the first published groundedness framework coupling evidence-typed scoring with tiered recovery under an explicit compute budget.

</details>


### [287] [Constraint-Based Analysis of Reasoning Shortcuts in Neurosymbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23377)
*Akihiro Takemura,Katsumi Inoue,Masaaki Nishino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了神经符号系统中的推理捷径问题，证明了区分性属性是避免捷径的必要但不充分条件，并提出了基于ASP的验证算法和贪婪修复算法，同时给出了复杂度分类和样本复杂度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号系统在学习过程中可能满足逻辑约束却未实现正确的概念-标签对应，即推理捷径问题。现有研究缺乏对捷径产生条件的严格形式化分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将推理捷径形式化为约束满足问题；证明区分性属性的必要性与不充分性；提出基于回答集编程(ASP)的验证算法和贪婪修复算法；进行复杂度分类（coNP-完备、#P-完备、NP-困难）；推导样本复杂度界限。

Result: 区分性属性在双射映射下是避免捷径的必要但不充分条件；ASP算法具有可靠且完备的验证能力；贪婪修复算法最多k次迭代收敛；捷径判定为coNP-完备，计数为#P-完备，最小修复为NP-困难；对数级别的标签查询在有利情况下足以消歧。

Conclusion: 本文为理解神经符号系统中的推理捷径提供了理论框架，并通过八个基准领域的实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems can satisfy logical constraints during learning without achieving the intended concept-label correspondence; this is a problem known as reasoning shortcuts. We formalize reasoning shortcuts as a constraint satisfaction problem and investigate under which conditions concept mappings are uniquely determined by the constraints. We prove that a discrimination property (requiring that no valid concept mapping can be transformed into another valid mapping by swapping two concept values) is necessary for shortcut-freeness under bijective mappings, but demonstrate via a counterexample that it is insufficient even when the constraint graph is connected. We develop an ASP-based algorithm that verifies whether a given constraint set uniquely determines the intended concept mapping, with proven soundness and completeness. When shortcuts are detected, a greedy repair algorithm eliminates them by augmenting the constraint set, converging in at most $k$ iterations, where $k$ is the number of alternative valid mappings. We further provide a complexity classification: deciding shortcut-freeness is coNP-complete, counting shortcuts is #P-complete, and finding minimal repairs is NP-hard. We also establish sample complexity bounds showing that logarithmically many label queries suffice for disambiguation in favorable cases, while querying all ambiguous positions suffices in the worst case. Experiments across eight benchmark domains validate our approach.

</details>


### [288] [SoccerRef-Agents: Multi-Agent System for Automated Soccer Refereeing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23392)
*Zi Meng,Wanli Song,Yi Hu,Jiayuan Rao,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SoccerRef-Agents，一个基于多智能体协作和跨模态RAG的足球裁判决策框架，通过构建基准、知识库和新型架构，显著提升裁判决策的准确性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助裁判方法局限于孤立视频感知任务，缺乏对犯规场景的理解与推理能力，无法满足体育裁判对公平、准确和可解释决策的核心需求。

Method: 构建了包含1200+裁判理论问题和600+犯规视频的多模态基准SoccerRefBench；基于最新《比赛规则》和经典案例库建立向量知识库RefKnowledgeDB；设计多智能体架构，通过跨模态RAG（检索增强生成）弥合视觉内容与规则文本之间的语义鸿沟。

Result: 在决策准确性和解释质量上显著优于通用多模态大模型（MLLMs）。

Conclusion: 该工作探索了将多模态大语言模型与裁判专业知识相结合的技术能力，证明专用框架在体育裁判任务上具有明显优势。

Abstract: Refereeing is vital in sports, where fair, accurate, and explainable decisions are fundamental. While intelligent assistant technologies are being widely adopted in soccer refereeing, current AI-assisted approaches remain preliminary. Existing research mostly focuses on isolated video perception tasks and lacks the ability to understand and reason about foul scenarios. To fill this gap, we propose SoccerRef-Agents, a holistic and explainable multi-agent decision-making framework for soccer refereeing. The main contributions are: (i) constructing the multimodal benchmark SoccerRefBench with over 1,200 referee theory questions and 600 foul video clips; (ii) building a vector-based knowledge base RefKnowledgeDB using the latest "Laws of the Game" and a classic case database for precise, knowledge-driven reasoning; (iii) designing a novel multi-agent architecture that collaborates via cross-modal RAG to bridge the semantic gap between visual content and regulatory texts. This work explores the technical capability of integrating MLLMs with refereeing expertise, and evaluations show our system significantly outperforms general-purpose MLLMs in decision accuracy and explanation quality. All databases, benchmarks, and code will be made available.

</details>


### [289] [When Corrective Hints Hurt: Prompt Design in Reasoner-Guided Repair of LLM Overcaution on Entailed Negations under OWL~2~DL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23398)
*Yijiashun Qi,Xiang Xu,Yuxuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GPT-5.4在OWL~2~DL查询中经常将"否"误答为"未知"。通过180条程序扩展查询和18条人工编写查询对比四种模式，发现单纯使用推理器验证的修复模式准确率达97.8%，而加入OWA提示反而下降至67.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到GPT-5.4在处理OWL~2~DL本体推理查询时存在系统性错误，特别是涉及FunctionalProperty闭包和类不相交性约束时，模型倾向于输出"未知"而非正确答案"否"。这种错误模式可复现，且对本体推理任务构成严重可靠性威胁。

Method: 基于180条按模式程序扩展的查询和18条两个无关领域（保险、临床）的人工编写查询，比较四种交互模式：单次查询、三轮通用"你错了"重试、三轮含开世界假设(OWA)提示的推理器裁决修复、三轮不含提示的推理器裁决修复。使用McNemar精确检验和Bonferroni校正进行统计比较。

Result: 单次查询准确率43.9%；通用重试提升至81.7%；带OWA提示的修复反而下降到67.2%；仅使用推理器裁决的修复达到97.8%。所有两两比较在α=0.01下均显著(p<10^{-5})。该错误模式也完全复现在4/4的留出查询上。

Conclusion: 提示框架比纠正性内容更重要；推理器引导的包装器应当进行明确的消融实验；加入OWA提示反而干扰模型表现，说明设计修复策略时需谨慎选择提示内容。

Abstract: We report a reproducible error pattern in GPT-5.4 on OWL~2~DL compliance queries: the model frequently answers ``unknown'' when the reasoner-entailed answer is ``no'' under \emph{FunctionalProperty} closure or class \emph{disjointness}. Using 180 reasoner-audited queries from a procedural expansion of the observed pattern plus 18 hand-authored held-out queries in two unrelated domains (insurance and clinical), we compare four interaction modes under matched query budget: single-shot, three rounds of generic ``you-are-wrong'' retry, three rounds of reasoner-verdict repair with an open-world-assumption (OWA) hint, and the same repair without the hint. Direct faithfulness is 43.9\,\% (Wilson 95\,\% CI $[36.8,51.2]$); generic retry reaches 81.7\,\% ($[75.4,86.6]$); the verdict-with-hint variant is \emph{worse} at 67.2\,\% ($[60.1,73.7]$); the verdict-only variant reaches 97.8\,\% ($[94.4,99.1]$). All pairwise comparisons remain significant under McNemar's exact test with Bonferroni correction ($α= 0.01$; all $p < 10^{-5}$). The same fingerprint accounts for 4/4 errors on the held-out queries. Our interpretation is bounded: prompt framing can matter more than corrective content, and reasoner-guided wrappers should be ablated explicitly.

</details>


### [290] [IndustryAssetEQA: A Neurosymbolic Operational Intelligence System for Embodied Question Answering in Industrial Asset Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23446)
*Chathurangi Shyalika,Dhaval Patel,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IndustryAssetEQA，一个结合时序遥测数据与故障模式知识图谱的神经符号系统，用于工业资产的具身问答。相比纯LLM基线，在结构有效性、反事实准确性和解释蕴含性上分别提升0.51、0.47和0.64，并将严重过度声明从28%降至2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的工业维护助手生成的解释缺乏遥测数据支撑、缺少可验证来源，且不支持反事实或行动导向推理，在安全关键场景中削弱了信任。

Method: 提出IndustryAssetEQA，采用神经符号方法，将时序遥测表示与故障模式影响分析知识图谱(FMEA-KG)相结合，实现工业资产的具身问答(EQA)能力。

Result: 在旋转机械、涡扇发动机、液压系统和网络物理生产系统四个数据集上，相比纯LLM基线，结构有效性提升0.51，反事实准确性提升0.47，解释蕴含性提升0.64，严重过度声明从28%降至2%（约93%的减少）。

Conclusion: IndustryAssetEQA通过融合遥测数据和领域知识图谱，显著提升了工业AI助手解释的可靠性、可验证性和推理能力，有效减少了过度声明问题。

Abstract: Industrial maintenance environments increasingly rely on AI systems to assist operators in understanding asset behavior, diagnosing failures, and evaluating interventions. Although large language models (LLMs) enable fluent natural-language interaction, deployed maintenance assistants routinely produce generic explanations that are weakly grounded in telemetry, omit verifiable provenance, and offer no testable support for counterfactual or action-oriented reasoning that undermine trust in safety-critical settings. We present IndustryAssetEQA, a neurosymbolic operational intelligence system that combines episodic telemetry representations with a Failure Mode Effects Analysis Knowledge Graph (FMEA-KG) to enable Embodied Question Answering (EQA) over industrial assets. We evaluate on four datasets covering four industrial asset types, including rotating machinery, turbofan engines, hydraulic systems, and cyber-physical production systems. Compared to LLM-only baselines, IndustryAssetEQA improves structural validity by up to 0.51, counterfactual accuracy by up to 0.47, and explanation entailment by 0.64, while reducing severe expert-rated overclaims from 28% to 2% (approximately 93% reduction). Code, datasets, and the FMEA-KG are available at https://github.com/IBM/AssetOpsBench/tree/IndustryAssetEQA/IndustryAssetEQA.

</details>
